# D5.5 Source Realignment Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Bring the Billing & Payment Service (repo `blooo-io/intelligent-eu-bps-source`, `develop` @ `6a24cc3`) in line with the project source documents and the arbitrations of 24/11/2026 (spec v6.1), and ship the D5.5 V1 package by 30/11/2026.

**Architecture:** Two-level billing: a monthly statement per participant (ontology `Billing`) over legal invoices per consumer→producer pair, issued in the producer's name and paid directly to the producer's Stripe Connect account. The platform never holds funds. Payouts go from the producer's connected account to their bank. Every integration with project services and the project UI goes through the EWDS Client Gateway: records are published, and actions arrive on a request/reply command channel. All real adapters are selected by environment, with mocks as the default.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, NestJS 11, React/Refine, PostgreSQL (CloudNativePG, JSONB journals), NATS JetStream + Debezium outbox, Temporal, APISIX, Keycloak, Stripe Connect + Identity, `pdf-lib`, vitest, pnpm 9, Node ≥ 22, Spectral/Redocly/AsyncAPI/oasdiff, Helm/Argo CD on Scaleway Kapsule (nl-ams).

**Spec:** vault `docs/requirements/` spec **v6.1** (items are the source of truth); arbitrations `plan/_ARBITRATIONS.md`; source baseline `extractions/_BASELINE.md`; gap audit `gap/G1..G3`.

## Global Constraints
- Contract first: OpenAPI/AsyncAPI/EWDS JSON schema change → mock → generated client → code (AGENTS.md §3.1).
- Module boundaries: no cross-module DB access; internal APIs or outbox events only; consumers idempotent on `eventId`.
- **The platform never holds or routes funds** (A1): charges are direct charges on the producer's connected account; no platform transfers; no platform balance.
- Legal invoice issuer = producer (self-billing mandate in the T&C); legal number sequential per producer, owned by billing; PDF generated by billing with `pdf-lib` (A4, A6). Stripe Invoicing is removed.
- Statement (Level 1) is informative, never payable (REQ-059).
- Early settlement is removed everywhere (REQ-057 deprecated); the per-pair threshold (default 5 EUR / 5 CHF) and the year-end / exit final run stay (A3).
- KYC: Connect onboarding for every participant role; Stripe Identity only for community managers; `kycVerified` gates statements, invoices, payments and payouts (A5).
- Project UI: EWDS only (published records + command channel); `/v1` REST serves `apps/web` only (A7). For sensitive entry (card/IBAN, 3-D Secure, SEPA mandate, KYC documents) the project UI chooses per request between a **hosted** Stripe page (reply carries a URL on `checkout.stripe.com`, `connect.stripe.com` or `verify.stripe.com`) and **embedded** Stripe components (reply carries a short-lived `clientSecret` + publishable key) — A7-bis. Card numbers, IBANs and identity documents never transit through billing or the EWDS.
- Adapters: **live EWDS in every deployed environment** (A8 revised 24/11): `MARKET_ADAPTER`, `DATASPACE_ADAPTER`, `ACTOR_SOURCE_ADAPTER`, `ERASURE_SOURCE_ADAPTER`, `TARIFF_ADAPTER` = `ewds` and `COMMAND_CHANNEL_ENABLED=true` in the Helm values of `dev` (and any later environment); `mock` only for local development and automated tests; no bilateral REST to GSY in production. If the EWDS rejects a call (missing role or topic), the adapter stays live, the failure is alerted (REQ-029) and logged as externally blocked — never switched back to mock. Keycloak unchanged (A9).
- No personal data in logs, events or EWDS payloads. Dependencies MIT / Apache-2.0 (plus Zlib and MIT-0, see decision B3).
- Per-module coverage ≥ 80 % (statements, branches, functions, lines); test names carry `REQ-xxx` / `UAT-xxx`.
- V1 journals are reset (test mode, no migration) (A10-6). Card + SEPA Direct Debit (A10-5). Live mode per community, Greenvolt first (A10-1).

## Arbitrations (validated 24/11/2026)
| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| A1 | Money goes directly from consumer to producer (direct charges); platform never transfers funds |
| A2 | Payout = connected account → bank, manual schedule; withdrawal by the producer or automatic per community; allowed once the period's invoices where they are producer are Paid |
| A3 | Per-pair threshold kept; early settlement removed |
| A4 | Legal invoice issued in the producer's name (self-billing) |
| A5 | Connect account for every participant; Stripe Identity only for managers |
| A6 | In-house PDF (`pdf-lib`), stored in DB, numbering per producer; Stripe Invoicing removed |
| A7 | Project UI through the EWDS only: records + request/reply commands, no link to Blooo |
| A8 | Real CGW adapters selected by env and **switched on (live EWDS) in all deployed environments**; mock only locally and in tests |
| A9 | Keycloak kept, no DID/VC work this sprint |
| A10 | Live mode per community; tariff port (0 when no tariff); optional `adjustedPrice`; EV charging out of scope; card + SEPA; journal reset; names removed from HEAD only |


## Amendment A7-bis (24/11/2026) — hosted OR embedded, chosen by the project UI per request
**This section overrides the text of WS3 Tasks 3.5-3.6, WS4 Tasks 4.2, 4.3, 4.16 and WS5 Task 5.7 wherever they assume hosted-only.**

Why Stripe UI is needed at all: card / IBAN entry (PCI-DSS: card data must go browser → Stripe directly), 3-D Secure strong customer authentication (PSD2), SEPA mandate acceptance, and KYC document + selfie capture (GDPR minimisation, no PII on the EWDS). Everything else is pure API over the EWDS.

**Command payloads** (WS4 Task 4.2) — `startPayment`, `startKyc` and the new `setupPaymentMethod` take `surface: "hosted" | "embedded"` (default `"hosted"`):
- `startPayment {invoiceId, surface, returnUrl?}` (`returnUrl` required when `surface = "hosted"`)
- `startKyc {surface, returnUrl?, refreshUrl?}`
- `setupPaymentMethod {surface, returnUrl?}` — saves a card or SEPA mandate for automatic payments (REQ-014).

**Reply `data`** (one schema, `oneOf` on `surface`):
```ts
type PspHandoff =
  | { surface: "hosted"; provider: "stripe"; purpose: "checkout" | "setup" | "connect_onboarding" | "identity";
      url: string /* https://(checkout|connect|verify).stripe.com/... */; expiresAt: string }
  | { surface: "embedded"; provider: "stripe"; purpose: "payment" | "setup" | "connect_onboarding" | "identity";
      publishableKey: string /* pk_test_ / pk_live_ per community billingMode */; clientSecret: string;
      connectedAccountRef?: string /* acct_… — Stripe.js `stripeAccount` option for direct charges and Connect components */;
      expiresAt: string };
// startPayment reply: { paymentId: string; handoff?: PspHandoff; status: "succeeded" | "processing" | "requires_action" }
// (no handoff when a saved method was charged off-session and no authentication is needed)
```
Embedded mapping: payment → PaymentIntent created **on the producer's connected account** (direct charge, card + `sepa_debit` via the Payment Element; 3-D Secure handled by Stripe.js); setup → SetupIntent; connect_onboarding → Account Session (`account_onboarding` component); identity → Identity VerificationSession `client_secret` (`stripe.verifyIdentity`). Hosted mapping: Checkout Session (mode `payment` or `setup`) on the producer account; Account Link; VerificationSession `url`.

**Interfaces (canonical):**
- WS5 `CollectPort.createEmbeddedPayment(input: { invoiceId; amount; currency; pspAccountRef; pspCustomerRef?; methods: ("card"|"sepa_debit")[]; idempotencyKey }): Promise<{ pspPaymentRef: string; clientSecret: string; expiresAt: string }>` — add to the shared contract suite (`REQ-014 embedded payment intent is created on the producer account and is idempotent`).
- WS5 `CollectPort.createPaymentMethodSetup(input: { participantId; surface: "hosted"|"embedded"; returnUrl? }): Promise<{ url?: string; clientSecret?: string; expiresAt: string }>` (replaces the embedded-only signature).
- WS5 `PaymentsService.startPayment(input: { invoiceId; actorId; surface: "hosted"|"embedded"; returnUrl? }): Promise<{ paymentId: string; status: "succeeded"|"processing"|"requires_action"; handoff?: PspHandoff }>` — replaces `{paymentId, checkoutUrl, expiresAt}` in WS4 Tasks 4.3/4.16.
- WS3 `startOnboarding({..., surface})` and `ManagerKycService.start(subjectId, communityId, surface, returnUrl?)` return a `PspHandoff`.
- WS3 Task 3.15 PSP registry exposes `publishableKeyFor(communityId): string` (test or live key by billingMode).

**Tests to add:**
- WS4 Task 4.2: schema fixtures — a valid `hosted` reply, a valid `embedded` reply; an embedded reply with a `url`, or a hosted reply whose host is not a Stripe domain, must FAIL validation.
- WS4 Task 4.16: `it("A7-bis: startPayment surface=embedded replies with clientSecret, publishableKey and connectedAccountRef")` and `it("A7-bis: startKyc surface=hosted replies with a connect.stripe.com URL")`.
- WS5 Task 5.7: `it("REQ-014 embedded card payment: PaymentIntent on the producer account, initiatedAt recorded")`.
- WS3 Task 3.6: `it("REQ-051 manager embedded identity returns a VerificationSession clientSecret")`.

**Security:** `clientSecret` and URLs are never logged, persisted or published on record topics; they travel only on the reply to the requesting gateway (B10). Publishable keys are public by design. The project UI integrates Stripe.js (embedded) or redirects (hosted) — its choice, per request.


## Amendment A8-live (24/11/2026) — EWDS adapters live in deployed environments
**Overrides WS4 wherever it says "default mock" for deployments, and A32 of the spec.**
- Code default of `adapterMode()` stays `mock` (local development and unit tests need no gateway), but every deployed environment sets the adapters to `ewds` and `COMMAND_CHANNEL_ENABLED=true`, and `INTAKE_BOOTSTRAP_S1=false`.
- WS4 Task 4.19 adds to `scripts/deploy-layout.test.mjs`: `it("A8-live: every deployed environment runs the EWDS adapters live")` — for each `deploy/envs/*` values file, assert `MARKET_ADAPTER`, `DATASPACE_ADAPTER`, `ACTOR_SOURCE_ADAPTER`, `ERASURE_SOURCE_ADAPTER`, `TARIFF_ADAPTER` equal `"ewds"`, `COMMAND_CHANNEL_ENABLED` equals `"true"` and `INTAKE_BOOTSTRAP_S1` equals `"false"`.
- The billing Client Gateway is configured with mTLS, its API key from OpenBao and its DID/VC roles (WS4 Task 4.19); roles or topics not yet granted by EWAG are logged as externally blocked in the progress file, with the adapter left live and the alert active.
- UAT results carry `run_path: live-cgw` when executed against the real gateway.

## Decisions taken while planning (defaults; flag to the Blooo product owner if you disagree)
| # | Issue raised by the plan | Default decision |
|---|---|---|
| B1 | Stripe holds manual-payout funds 90 days at most | Forced automatic payout of the available balance at day 80 after the funds become available, whatever the debtors' status; operator alert at day 70 |
| B2 | Grid fees on a pair invoice would be collected on the producer's account | Per-community `tariffBilling` setting, default **off** (grid fee 0 + mention) until a legal check; calculation code ships |
| B3 | `pdf-lib` pulls `pako` (MIT AND Zlib); `nodemailer` is MIT-0 | Accept: permissive OSI licences; amend TP-2 to "MIT, Apache-2.0, BSD-2/3, ISC, Zlib, MIT-0" |
| B4 | The command channel trusts the `actorId` asserted by the project-UI gateway | Allowlist of caller gateway DIDs + role check against the participants registry; documented residual risk; ask EWAG for signed end-user assertions (D5.10) |
| B5 | Stripe keeps KYC data under AML law after erasure | Erasure reports `retained_by_psp` for the connected account; stated in the D5.5 report |
| B6 | Removing `POST /v1/early-settlements` is a breaking change | Remove from `/v1` with an oasdiff waiver: V1 not yet submitted, only `apps/web` consumes it |
| B7 | A saved SEPA mandate cannot be cloned onto the producer's account | SEPA (and card without a saved method) is paid in a session on the producer's account: Checkout Session (`surface: hosted`) or Payment Element on a PaymentIntent (`surface: embedded`), per A7-bis |
| B8 | Gapless numbering per producer under concurrency | Number assigned inside the issue transaction, unique index (issuer, number) with retry, invoicing deployed with `replicaCount: 1` (chart guard test) — as in WS2 Task 2.8 |
| B9 | Two pods sharing one CGW client consume each other's messages | One replica for each EWDS-consuming deployment in V1 (trade-intake, publication command poller, participants Actor intake, compliance erasure intake) |
| B10 | Stripe URLs travel on the EWDS reply channel | Accept (short-lived, single-use URLs); ask EWAG for payload encryption on the reply channel |
| B11 | Due date | 30/11/2026 (project start 01/12/2024 on the deliverable cover pages); confirm with R2M |

## Review Focus
- A consumer pays an invoice twice (double click, webhook replay): one charge only — covered by the contract suite test `UAT-014 REQ-014 direct card charge succeeds and is idempotent` (WS5 Task 5.3/5.4); add to Task 5.7: `it("REQ-014 payInvoice twice on the same open invoice creates one charge")` asserting `collect.createDirectCharge` called once and the second call returns the first `paymentId`.
- A producer withdraws while one invoice of the period is still Open: refused with reason `producer_invoices_unpaid` — test `A2: one unpaid producer invoice blocks the period` in WS1 Task 1.15.
- A community switches from virtual to live with an open period: refused 409 `open_period` — test in WS3 Task 3.14 (needs `GET /internal/v1/communities/{id}/open-periods`, added to WS1 Task 1.2: `it("REQ-043 open-periods lists periods with unbilled or open invoices")`).
- An EWDS command from an unknown gateway DID or for an actor of another community: rejected `forbidden`, no internal call — tests in WS4 Task 4.15 (`A7: unknown sender DID -> forbidden, no internal call`).
- Two invoices for the same producer issued concurrently: consecutive numbers, no gap, no duplicate — tests in WS2 Task 2.8 (`REQ-042 UAT-053 next number is per producer and gapless`, `REQ-042 unique index rejects a duplicate issuer/number`).

## Workstream 1 — Billing model: monthly participant statement, pair invoices kept, early settlement removed, payouts

Keep the pair engine (netting per pair, per-pair threshold and carry-forward, forced final run) and delete early settlement end to end (API, service, web, seeds, mocks, EWDS enum).
Add level 1 of the two-level model: one monthly **participant statement** (ontology `Billing`) per participant and period, owned by **invoicing (AC-06)** and issued after each billing run.
Add **payouts** in payments (AC-07): a Connect account for every role, a manual payout schedule, withdrawal or `autoPayout`, a gate on paid producer invoices, `payout.*` webhooks, payout records and events, plus the manager overview (REQ-060) and the participant payouts view. Then reset the V1 journals.

### Decisions taken in this workstream (state them in the PR descriptions)

**D1. Invoicing owns the statement, not settlement.**
- The statement lists `invoiceIds` and their status. Invoicing is the single master of both (TP-7). Settlement finishes before any invoice exists.
- Invoicing does not invoice zero-amount rows (carry-forward). It also holds pairs back while the producer is not ready (BR-09). Settlement sees neither outcome.
- If settlement owned the statement, settlement → invoicing → settlement would form a cycle of reads and events.
- The statement status follows `InvoiceStatusChanged`, which is local to AC-06, so no event hop is needed.
- The payout gate (AC-07) reads one place: `GET /internal/v1/statements`.

**D2. Every participant (consumer, producer, prosumer) gets a Connect account. The per-producer cloned Customers are removed, but per-charge PaymentMethod cloning stays.** These Stripe constraints drive the choice:
- A direct charge needs a PaymentMethod that exists on the payee's connected account.
- Stripe clones PaymentMethods only from the **platform** to a connected account, and only of type `card` or `us_bank_account`. This is in the Stripe doc "Share payment methods across multiple accounts for direct charges". The repo already hits the SEPA limit in `stripe-psp.adapter.ts:353-361`, with `sepa_debit_not_shareable`.
- So a PaymentMethod saved on the consumer's own Connect account can never pay a producer's account. The consumer's Connect account is used only for KYC (A5) and for receiving money and payouts when the consumer also sells (prosumer, role change).
- Same Stripe doc: "If you don't collect recurring payments, you don't need to create a Customer and persistent PaymentMethod on the connected account… clone the PaymentMethod for each direct charge". The clone is consumed by one PaymentIntent.
- Stripe Invoicing is removed (A6), so nothing else needs a Customer on the producer account. Result: `connected-customers.store.ts`, `ensureConnectedCustomer` and `POST /internal/v1/connected-customers:ensure` are deleted. `createPayment` becomes "clone the platform card, then create and confirm a PaymentIntent on the payee account".
- SEPA Direct Debit (A10.5) cannot go through this path. It needs the Stripe-hosted Checkout Session created on the producer account (owner: the `startPayment` command workstream; see Dependencies).

**D3. Payout amount and gate.**
- A payout covers one `(participantId, period)` pair.
- Gate: a statement exists for that participant and period, and every non-Void invoice in it where the participant is the **producer** has status `Paid`.
- Amount: Σ(`amount − pspFee`) over the succeeded payments on those invoices whose payee is this participant. An invoice marked Paid manually (offline payment) adds 0, because its money never reached Stripe.
- Stripe-side check: `available balance ≥ amount`, otherwise the request gets 409 `funds_pending`.
- The payout is created on the connected account (`Stripe-Account` header). There is no transfer and no platform balance (A1).

### Task 1.6: Invoicing domain — statement computation

**Files:**
- Create `services/invoicing/src/domain/statement.ts` and `statement.spec.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
```ts
export type StatementStatus = "Open" | "PartiallyPaid" | "Paid" | "Overdue" | "Void";
export type StatementInvoiceView = Pick<InvoiceRecord, "invoiceId" | "producerId" | "consumerId" | "total" | "status">;
export type StatementTotals = { totalBought: string; totalSold: string; netBalance: string;
  direction: "to_pay" | "to_receive" | "balanced"; invoiceIds: string[]; status: StatementStatus };
export function computeStatement(participantId: string, invoices: StatementInvoiceView[]): StatementTotals;
export function deriveStatementStatus(statuses: InvoiceStatus[]): StatementStatus;
```
- Approach: add up totals in integer cents (BigInt parse of `"12.34"`, never `Number`). Status rules, in order:
  1. Void invoices are ignored; if every invoice is Void → `Void`.
  2. Any `Overdue` → `Overdue`.
  3. All `Paid` → `Paid`.
  4. Any `Paid` or `PartiallyPaid` → `PartiallyPaid`.
  5. Otherwise → `Open` (Draft and Disputed count as unpaid).

**Covers:** REQ-059, UAT-076.

- [ ] Write the failing test:
```ts

it("UAT-076 / REQ-059: one statement nets sales to A and B against a purchase from C", () => {
  const s = computeStatement("P", [inv("i1","P","A","12.00"), inv("i2","P","B","8.50"), inv("i3","C","P","6.25")]);
  expect(s).toEqual({ totalBought: "6.25", totalSold: "20.50", netBalance: "14.25", direction: "to_receive", invoiceIds: ["i1","i2","i3"], status: "Open" });
});
it("REQ-059: negative net is to_pay", () => {
  expect(computeStatement("P", [inv("i3","C","P","6.25")]).netBalance).toBe("-6.25");
  expect(computeStatement("P", [inv("i3","C","P","6.25")]).direction).toBe("to_pay");
});
it("status: Paid+Open → PartiallyPaid; Paid+Void → Paid; Overdue wins; all Void → Void", () => {
  expect(deriveStatementStatus(["Paid","Open"])).toBe("PartiallyPaid");
  expect(deriveStatementStatus(["Paid","Void"])).toBe("Paid");
  expect(deriveStatementStatus(["Paid","Overdue"])).toBe("Overdue");
  expect(deriveStatementStatus(["Void"])).toBe("Void");
  expect(deriveStatementStatus(["Disputed"])).toBe("Open");
});
it("sums 0.10 + 0.20 exactly", () => {
  expect(computeStatement("P", [inv("a","P","A","0.10"), inv("b","P","B","0.20")]).totalSold).toBe("0.30");
});
```

### Task 1.7: Invoicing — issue statements after each billing run

**Files:**
- `services/invoicing/src/domain/types.ts`: `InvoiceRecord` gains `runId: string`, `period: string`, `periodFrom: string`, `periodUntil: string`.
- `services/invoicing/src/invoicing/invoicing.service.ts:337-358`: copy those fields from `PairSettlementInput`. `PairSettlementInput` gains `period?: string`, mapped in `settlement-client.port.ts` from `body.period`.
- Create `domain/statement-store.ts`.
- `domain/store.ts:4`: `InvoiceWrite = { kind: "invoice"; row: InvoiceRecord } | { kind: "statement"; row: StatementRecord }`.
- `persistence/invoice-journal.ts:19-42`: add the `statements (statement_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, participant_id TEXT NOT NULL, period TEXT NOT NULL, payload JSONB NOT NULL)` table, upsert and hydrate.
- Create `invoicing/statements.service.ts` (+ spec).
- `events/settlement-completed.listener.ts:33-62`.
- `app.module.ts` providers.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `computeStatement` (Task 1.6); `SettlementCompleted.data.runId` (Task 1.1).
- Produces:
```ts
export type StatementRecord = StatementTotals & { statementId: string; participantId: string; communityId: string;
  period: string; periodFrom: string; periodUntil: string; currency: string; revision: number; issuedAt: string; updatedAt: string };
export class StatementStore { upsert(row: StatementRecord): void; find(participantId: string, communityId: string, period: string): StatementRecord | undefined;
  listByParticipant(participantIds: string[]): StatementRecord[]; listByInvoice(invoiceId: string): StatementRecord[]; reset(): void }
export class StatementsService {
  issueStatementsForRun(runId: string): Promise<StatementRecord[]>;
  recomputeForInvoice(invoiceId: string): Promise<void>;   // Task 1.8
  listForParticipant(sub: string): Promise<StatementRecord[]>;
  listInternal(participantId: string, period?: string): Promise<Array<StatementRecord & { invoices: StatementInvoiceView[] }>>;
}
```
- Approach for `issueStatementsForRun`:
  1. Take the invoices with `runId`.
  2. Group them per (participant ∈ {producer, consumer}, communityId, period).
  3. Merge with all earlier invoices of the same participant and period. A final or correction run in the same period adds to the statement and does not duplicate it.
  4. Upsert with `revision + 1` only when the totals or `invoiceIds` change.
  5. Append outbox `StatementIssued` (aggregateType `Statement`), all inside `this.tx.run`.
- The listener calls it after the pair loop. It also calls it on the non-retryable failure branch (l.53-59), so the invoices that were created still get a statement.

**Covers:** REQ-059, REQ-054, UAT-076, NFR-015.

### Task 1.15: Payments domain — payout gate and amount

**Files:**
- Create `services/payments/src/domain/payout.ts` and `payout.spec.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
```ts
export type PayoutStatementView = { participantId: string; period: string; currency: string;
  invoices: Array<{ invoiceId: string; producerId: string; consumerId: string; total: string; status: string }> };
export type PayoutPaymentView = { invoiceId: string; payeeId?: string; status: string; amount: string; pspFee?: string };
export type PayoutDecision =
  | { eligible: true; amount: string; invoiceIds: string[] }
  | { eligible: false; reason: "statement_not_found" | "producer_invoices_unpaid" | "nothing_to_pay_out"; unpaidInvoiceIds?: string[] };
export function decidePayout(input: { participantId: string; statement: PayoutStatementView | null; payments: PayoutPaymentView[] }): PayoutDecision;
```
- Approach: producer invoices = `invoices.filter(i => i.producerId === participantId && i.status !== "Void")`. Every one of them must be `Paid`. Amount = Σ cents(`amount − (pspFee ?? "0.00")`) over the `succeeded` payments on those invoices with `payeeId === participantId`. Amount 0 → `nothing_to_pay_out`.

**Covers:** REQ-015, UAT-015, A2 (gate).

- [ ] Write the failing test:
```ts

  invoices: [{ invoiceId: "iA", producerId: "P", consumerId: "A", total: "12.00", status: statuses[0][1] },
             { invoiceId: "iB", producerId: "P", consumerId: "B", total: "8.50", status: statuses[1][1] },
             { invoiceId: "iC", producerId: "C", consumerId: "P", total: "6.25", status: "Open" }] });
const pays = [{ invoiceId: "iA", payeeId: "P", status: "succeeded", amount: "12.00", pspFee: "0.43" },
              { invoiceId: "iB", payeeId: "P", status: "succeeded", amount: "8.50", pspFee: "0.37" }];

});
```

### Task 1.16: Payments — withdrawal request, payout records, events, member API

**Files:**
- Create `domain/payouts.store.ts`, `persistence/payout-journal.ts` (table `payouts (payout_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, participant_id TEXT NOT NULL, period TEXT NOT NULL, payload JSONB NOT NULL)`), `ports/statement-lookup.port.ts` (HTTP via `INVOICING_INTERNAL_URL`, plus in-memory), `payouts/payouts.service.ts`, `payouts/payouts.controller.ts` (+ specs).
- `app.module.ts`.
- `domain/store.ts:1-19`: `PaymentRecord.pspFee?: string` is read only; it is written by the KPI workstream.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `decidePayout`, `PspPort.getAvailableBalance/createPayout`, `PaymentsStore.getAccount`, payments by invoice id.
- Produces:
```ts
export type PayoutStatus = "requested" | "in_transit" | "paid" | "failed" | "canceled";
export type PayoutRecord = { payoutId: string; participantId: string; communityId: string; period: string; amount: string; currency: string;
  status: PayoutStatus; trigger: "participant" | "auto"; attempt: number; pspPayoutRef: string | null; invoiceIds: string[];
  requestedAt: string; arrivalDate?: string; failureCategory?: string };
export interface StatementLookupPort { list(participantId: string, period?: string): Promise<PayoutStatementView[]> }
export const STATEMENT_LOOKUP = Symbol("STATEMENT_LOOKUP");
export class PayoutsService {
  requestWithdrawal(input: { participantId: string; communityId: string; period: string; trigger: "participant" | "auto" }): Promise<PayoutRecord>;
  listForParticipant(participantId: string): Promise<{ items: PayoutRecord[]; withdrawable: Array<{ period: string; amount: string; currency: string }> }>;
}
```
- Approach for `requestWithdrawal`, all inside `tx.run`:
  1. Resolve the caller.
  2. The account must have `kycStatus === "verified"` and a Connect id, else 409 `kyc_required`.
  3. A payout of that period in `requested | in_transit | paid` already exists → return it.
  4. Run `decidePayout`. Not eligible → 409 `{ reason }`.
  5. `getAvailableBalance` below the amount → 409 `funds_pending`.
  6. Persist a `requested` record, then call `createPayout` with `idempotencyKey = payout:<participantId>:<period>:<attempt>`.
  7. Store `pspPayoutRef` and the status.
  8. Outbox `PayoutStatusChanged`.
- `requestWithdrawal` is the method the EWDS command-channel handler calls for `requestWithdrawal` (A7).

**Covers:** REQ-015, REQ-060 (participant view), UAT-015.

- [ ] Write failing tests:
```ts
it("UAT-015: a verified producer withdraws 19.70 for 2026-10; one PayoutStatusChanged", async () => {
  psp.balance = "25.00";
  const p = await svc.requestWithdrawal({ participantId: "P", communityId: COM, period: "2026-10", trigger: "participant" });
  expect(p).toMatchObject({ amount: "19.70", currency: "EUR", status: "in_transit", attempt: 1, invoiceIds: ["iA", "iB"] });
  expect(psp.createPayout).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ accountRef: "acct_P", amount: "19.70", idempotencyKey: "payout:P:2026-10:1" }));
```

### Task 1.17: Payments — `payout.*` webhooks update payout status

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `PayoutsService.applyPayoutWebhook(payload: { payoutRef: string; status: PayoutStatus; accountRef: string; arrivalDate?: string; failureCategory?: string }): Promise<void>`. It finds the record by `pspPayoutRef`, checks `accountRef` against the participant's account, and emits `PayoutStatusChanged` only when the status changes.
- Terminal states (`paid`, `failed`, `canceled`) are never left. Exception: Stripe sends `payout.failed` after `paid` (a bank return), which moves `paid → failed`.

**Covers:** REQ-015, UAT-015, NFR (idempotency on Stripe event id, 04 §7).

- Specs.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `PayoutsService.runAutoPayouts(input: { communityId: string }): Promise<{ requested: number; blocked: number; nearHoldingLimit: string[] }>`.
  - Returns early with zeros when `autoPayout` is false.
  - Otherwise, for each verified account of the community and each statement with a positive `totalSold` and no live payout, it calls `requestWithdrawal({ trigger: "auto" })` and counts 409s as `blocked`.
  - `nearHoldingLimit` lists the payout-eligible participant ids whose oldest unpaid-out succeeded payment is ≥ 75 days old. Stripe's manual-payout holding limit is 90 days outside the US/TH.
- Consumes: `CommunitySettings.autoPayout` (Task 1.11).

**Covers:** REQ-015 (automatic per community), A2.

---

#### Review focus (Workstream 1)

1. **Payout double-spend across periods.** Stripe's available balance is account-wide. Two periods could each pass the `available ≥ amount` check against the same funds. Test added to Task 1.16: `"two eligible periods 19.70 + 4.80 with available 20.00: the second request is 409 funds_pending"`. The check uses `available − Σ(in_transit payouts not yet debited)`.
2. **Statement missed when invoices are created outside a run event** (operator bridge `POST /internal/v1/invoices:from-settlement`). Test added to Task 1.7: `"invoices:from-settlement for run R3 issues the statement for R3"`. The bridge calls `issueStatementsForRun(runId)` after creating the invoice.
3. **Refund after payout.** A refund on a producer invoice (REQ-016 flow) after the payout was paid can leave the account balance negative. Test added to Task 1.17: `"charge.refunded on a paid-out invoice records failureCategory 'refund_after_payout' on the PayoutProjection and keeps the payout paid"`. This is reported, not reversed. Stripe recovers it from future funds (`losses.payments=stripe`, `fees.payer=account`).
4. **Stripe 90-day manual-payout holding limit (EU/CH)** while debtors do not pay. Test added to Task 1.18 (`nearHoldingLimit`). The policy at day 90 needs <name>'s arbitration: forced partial payout or a manager reminder. Today the code only flags it.
5. **Currency mismatch.** A CHF community with an EUR-only connected-account balance (Swiss accounts settle in CHF; FX at Stripe). Test added to Task 1.12: `"getAvailableBalance returns '0.00' when the currency is absent"`. Task 1.16 then refuses with `funds_pending` rather than paying the wrong currency.

#### Dependencies

**Needs from other workstreams:**
- **Invoicing PDF / Stripe Invoicing removal (A6):**
  - It deletes `StripeInvoicePort`, `InvoicingService.resolveConnectedCustomer` and `resolveProducerStripeAccount`, and `PaymentsClientPort.ensureConnectedCustomer`, before or with Task 1.14.
  - It keeps `InvoiceRecord.producerId/consumerId/total/status` and the new `runId/period/periodFrom/periodUntil` (Task 1.7).
  - The `InvoiceWrite` union (Task 1.7) must be merged with any record change that workstream makes.
- **KPI / PSP fee (ER1.4, TE-2):** `PaymentRecord.pspFee?: string` (Stripe `balance_transaction.fee`) set on payment success. Without it Task 1.15 treats the fee as `"0.00"` and overstates the payout; Stripe then refuses it through `funds_pending`.
- **PSP-neutral port:** the rename of the remaining `stripe*` names. New WS1 names are already neutral (`accountRef`, `payoutRef`, `payeeAccountRef`, `payerPaymentMethodRef`).
- **KYC (A5):** `kycVerified` publication (`billingKycStatus`) and Stripe Identity for managers. WS1 Task 1.13 only creates the Connect account for every role and gates readiness.
- **EWDS command channel / publication (A7):**
  - The `requestWithdrawal` command handler calls `PayoutsService.requestWithdrawal({ trigger: "participant" })`.
  - `startPayment` provides the Stripe-hosted Checkout Session on the producer account for SEPA, and for cards when no saved card exists (D2).
  - Publication maps `StatementIssued`/`StatementStatusChanged` → `billingStatement` and `PayoutStatusChanged` → `billingPayout`.
- **Notification:** templates `payout-paid`, `payout-failed` and an operator alert for `nearHoldingLimit` (consume `PayoutStatusChanged`).
- **Docs/spec v6.1:** AGENTS.md §1 ("early settlement is possible", "Stripe is master of invoices"), architecture views (BR-05 early clause, F-processes), UAT-074 marked deprecated.

**Provides to other workstreams:**
- Events:
  - `StatementIssued { statementId, participantId, period, periodFrom, periodUntil, totalBought, totalSold, netBalance, currency, invoiceIds, status, revision }`
  - `StatementStatusChanged { statementId, participantId, period, status }`
  - `PayoutStatusChanged { payoutId, participantId, period, amount, currency, status, failureCategory? }`
- `GET /internal/v1/statements?participantId&period` (AC-06).
- `PayoutsService.requestWithdrawal(...)`, `PayoutsService.runAutoPayouts({ communityId })`.
- `PspPort.createConnectedAccount / getAvailableBalance / createPayout`, `PAYOUT_WEBHOOK_EVENTS`.
- `CommunitySettings.autoPayout`.
- `BillingRunType = "periodic" | "final"`.
- Public operations: `listMyStatements`, `listMyPayouts`, `requestWithdrawal`, `getPayoutsOverview`.
## Workstream 2 — Invoices: in-house PDF, numbering, tariffs, price adjustment, notifications

Replace Stripe Invoicing in AC-06 with an invoice issued by billing: a legal number per producer, gapless and immutable, assigned at issue; a PDF rendered with `pdf-lib` and stored in the invoicing DB; an e-mail with the PDF attached, sent by AC-11 (A6).
Add the calculation inputs the invoice must show: a `TariffPort` in AC-13 (mock adapter, plus the EWDS source port that WS4 implements), grid fee, levy and tax computed in AC-05 or 0 with a mention (A10.2, REQ-004), and an optional `adjustedPrice` per trade (A10.3). Also fix the FX rate precision bug (2 decimals instead of 4 to 6) and publish real `gridFeeAmount`/`taxAmount`.
Add payment reminders: a manager action plus a scheduled reminder after Overdue, and a missing-payment alert to the community manager (REQ-061). Fix the latent bug where the scheduled `MarkOverdue` call never sends `asOf`.

Repo paths are relative to `repo/` (develop @6a24cc3). Line ranges refer to that snapshot. Spec ids follow v6.1: REQ-039 and REQ-042 cover the in-house PDF with per-producer numbering; REQ-054 is the pair invoice in the producer's name, reactivated under REQ-059; REQ-050 is the T&C including the self-billing mandate.

**Calculation rules fixed by this workstream (reviewers check against these):**
- R1. The effective price of a trade is `adjustedPrice ?? price` (EUR/kWh). The line shows both values when `adjustedPrice` is present. REQ-003 still holds: billing never computes an adjustment, it only uses the one GSY sends.
- R2. Tariff charges are computed per pair in canonical orientation, before the threshold decision. Each charge is `Σ signedKWh × perKWh`, times the FX rate when the tariff is in EUR and the community uses CHF, and each component is rounded once to the cent. Charges enter the pair amount, so they are netted and carried like energy (BR-04/BR-05).
- R3. On an invoiced pair, `energyAmount = invoiced − gridFee − levy − tax`, which includes carried-in. VAT is `round(invoiced × vatRate)` and total is `invoiced + VAT`. A carried pair shows 0 charges, and its charges ride in the carry.
- R4. With no tariff, all three components are `0.00`, `tariffId` is `null`, and the PDF prints the REQ-004 mention.
- R5. The legal number is `SB-<first 8 hex of producerId, upper case>-<6-digit sequence>`. There is one series per producer, it never resets, it is assigned inside the issue transaction (rolled back with it, so no gaps), and it never changes afterwards.
- R6. On EWDS, `gridFeeAmount = gridFee + levy` and `taxAmount = totalTaxAmount = tax + VAT`. The legal number is never published (REQ-042).

### Task 2.1: Contracts — calculation inputs and EWDS grid fee
**Files:** `contracts/openapi/components/schemas.yaml` (L29-66 `InvoiceTradeLine`, `FxRate`; L112-126 `JobName`), `contracts/openapi/internal/reference-data.v1.yaml` (L18-44, L100-115), `contracts/openapi/internal/trade-intake.v1.yaml` (L151-182), `contracts/openapi/internal/settlement.v1.yaml` (L147-200), `docs/architecture/ewds-topics/billingInvoice.v1.0.0.schema.json` (L92-122), `mocks/schemas/billingInvoice.v1.0.0.schema.json`, `docs/architecture/ewds-topics/billingInvoice.example.json`

### Task 2.4: TariffPort, mock adapter, EWDS source port, tariff endpoint (AC-13)
**Files:** create `services/reference-data/src/tariff/tariff.port.ts`, `mock-tariff.adapter.ts`, `tariff.controller.ts`, and `tariff.spec.ts`; modify `services/reference-data/src/app.module.ts` (L9-15)
**Interfaces:** Produces
```ts
export type Tariff = { tariffId: string; communityId: string; currency: "EUR" | "CHF";
  validFrom: string; validUntil: string | null;           // YYYY-MM-DD, until exclusive
  gridFeePerKWh: string; levyPerKWh: string; taxPerKWh: string; source: "mock" | "ewds" };
export interface TariffPort { getTariff(communityId: string, date: string): Promise<Tariff | null>; }
export const TARIFF_PORT: unique symbol;
/** Transport implemented by WS4 (EWDS Client Gateway subscription to the ontology Tariff topic). */
export type EwdsTariffRecord = { tariffId: string; communityId: string; currency: string; validFrom: string;
  validUntil?: string | null; gridFeePerKWh: number; levyPerKWh: number; taxPerKWh: number };
export interface EwdsTariffSource { findTariff(communityId: string, date: string): Promise<EwdsTariffRecord | null>; }
export const EWDS_TARIFF_SOURCE: unique symbol;
export class EwdsTariffAdapter implements TariffPort { constructor(source: EwdsTariffSource) }
export function createTariffPort(env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv, source?: EwdsTariffSource | null): TariffPort;
export const UAT004_TARIFF_COMMUNITY_ID = "a2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004";
```
- `TariffController.getTariff(communityId, date)` returns `{communityId, date, tariff}`.
- `MockTariffAdapter` holds a single fixture: `{tariffId:"tariff-uat004-2026", communityId: UAT004_TARIFF_COMMUNITY_ID, currency:"EUR", validFrom:"2026-01-01", validUntil:null, gridFeePerKWh:"0.0450", levyPerKWh:"0.0100", taxPerKWh:"0.0050", source:"mock"}`.
- `TARIFF_PROVIDER=mock` (default) or `ewds`. `ewds` without an injected source throws `"EWDS tariff source not configured (WS4)"` at boot.

```ts
export type TariffQuote = { tariffId: string; currency: "EUR" | "CHF"; gridFeePerKWh: string; levyPerKWh: string; taxPerKWh: string };
export type TariffCharges = { gridFee: Dec; levy: Dec; tax: Dec; tariffId: string | null };
export function computeTariffCharges(input: { trades: CanonicalTrade[]; tariff: TariffQuote | null;
  communityCurrency: "EUR" | "CHF"; fxRate: Dec }): TariffCharges;          // canonical orientation

getTariff(communityId: string, date: string): Promise<TariffQuote | null>;
```

```ts
export type InvoiceParty = { participantId: string; name: string; address: string };

  status: InvoiceStatus; statusAudit: InvoiceStatusAudit[];
  legalNumber: string; issuedAt: string; dueDate: string;
  periodFrom: string | null; periodUntil: string | null; settlementType: "periodic" | "early" | "final";
  issuer: InvoiceParty; addressee: InvoiceParty; mandate: SelfBillingMandate;

  amounts: InvoiceAmounts; total: string; vatRate: number; carriedForwardIn: string;
  exchangeRate: { rate: string; rateDate: string } | null; tariffId: string | null;
  document: { sha256: string; byteLength: number } | null;
  reminders: Array<{ at: string; trigger: "manual" | "scheduled"; actorId: string }> };
// InvoiceStatusAudit.source: "manual" | "payment" | "mark_overdue"   ("stripe" removed)
export function formatLegalNumber(producerId: string, seq: number): string;   // R5
export function legalSequence(legalNumber: string): number;
export function sumMoney(...values: string[]): string;                        // integer cents, "-0.00" → "0.00"
export type InvoiceWrite = { kind: "row"; row: InvoiceRecord } | { kind: "document"; invoiceId: string; sha256: string; content: Buffer };
// InvoiceStore
nextLegalNumber(producerId: string): string;
putDocument(invoiceId: string, sha256: string, content: Buffer): void;
getDocument(invoiceId: string): Buffer | undefined;
```

The `InvoiceIssued` data matches `{ amounts:{energy:"2.00",gridFee:"0.45",levy:"0.10",tax:"0.05",vat:"0.26",total:"2.86"}, tariffApplied:true }` and has no `legalNumber` key.

- [ ] Implement, in order inside `tx.run`:
  1. idempotency check;
  2. zero skip;
  3. readiness check;
  4. parties (missing producer or mandate throws `"producer not ready: self-billing mandate missing"`; missing consumer throws `"producer not ready: consumer billing profile missing"`, retryable);
  5. `legalNumber = store.nextLegalNumber(producerId)`;
  6. build the record (`sumMoney` for the total; due date = issue + 14 days);
  7. `bytes = await renderer(record)`;
  8. `put` then `putDocument`;
  9. outbox.

### Task 2.16: Reminders in AC-06 (manual and scheduled) and the MarkOverdue `asOf` fix
**Files:** `services/invoicing/src/invoicing/invoicing.service.ts` (add after L419), `public.controller.ts` (add after L134), `invoicing.controller.ts` (add after L174); `services/scheduler/src/domain/types.ts` (L2-13), `domain/catalog.ts` (L12-47), `schedules/catalog.ts` (after L59), `scheduler/scheduler.service.ts` (L172-173), `temporal/workflows.ts` (after L63), plus their specs
**Interfaces:** Produces
```ts
sendReminder(invoiceId: string, input: { actorId: string; communityId?: string; trigger: "manual" | "scheduled" }):
  Promise<{ invoiceId: string; reminderCount: number }>;      // 409 ConflictException unless Open | Overdue | PartiallyPaid
sendDueReminders(asOf: string): Promise<{ accepted: true; sent: number }>;
```
- `sendDueReminders` targets Overdue invoices. It sends when there is no scheduled reminder yet, or when the last one was `REMINDER_INTERVAL_DAYS` (default 7) or more days before `asOf`. It sends at most `REMINDER_MAX` (default 3) scheduled reminders.
- Outbox: `PaymentReminderRequested` (Task 2.2 schema) plus `AuditableActionPerformed {action:"invoice.reminder.manual"}` for the manual trigger.
- Scheduler:
  - job `SendReminders`: owner AC-06, path `/invoices:send-reminders`, id `a1000000-0000-4000-8000-00000000000c`;
  - schedule `intelligent.send-reminders.daily`, cron `0 9 * * *`;
  - body `{communityId, asOf: today}`;
  - also `MarkOverdue` body `{communityId, asOf: today}`.

**Covers:** REQ-061, UAT-078, REQ-044

  - `"REQ-061 refuses a reminder on a Paid invoice"`: 409.
  - `"REQ-049 manager of another community gets 404"`.
  - `"REQ-061 scheduled reminders every 7 days, at most 3"`: an invoice Overdue since 2026-12-01 gives `sendDueReminders("2026-12-02").sent === 1`, `("2026-12-05") === 0`, `("2026-12-09") === 1`, `("2026-12-16") === 1`, `("2026-12-23") === 0`.
  - Scheduler: `"MarkOverdue and SendReminders carry asOf"`: `bodyFor("MarkOverdue")` matches `{ communityId, asOf: /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/ }`; the catalogue contains `SendReminders` with path `/invoices:send-reminders`.

#### Review focus (Workstream 2)
1. **Two replicas would break gapless numbering.** The in-memory `nextLegalNumber` assumes one writer (the chart sets `replicaCount: 1`). Added to Task 2.8: `"REQ-042 unique index rejects a duplicate issuer/number"`. The fake SQL raises `23505`, `applyInvoiceWrites` rejects, the store is restored, and a retry takes the next free number. Add a chart guard in `scripts/deploy-layout.test.mjs` asserting `invoicing.replicaCount === 1`.
2. **Grid fee collected on the producer's Connect account.** With a real tariff, DSO money would be paid to the producer, so it is a flow-of-funds and legal question (A1). Added to Task 2.7: `"REQ-004 a tariff is applied only when the community enables tariffBilling"`. UAT-004 community with `tariffBilling: false` gives zero charges and `tariffId null`. The UAT-004 fixture community sets it to true. Raise this with legal before UAT-004 is marked live.
3. **Negative net kWh with positive energy.** Price differences between the two directions can make the netted kWh negative while the energy amount is positive, which produces negative charges. Added to Task 2.7: `"REQ-004 charges are never negative on an invoiced pair"`. Each component is clamped at 0 after orientation, and a `DiscrepancyDetected` outbox event is emitted.
4. **PDF and record drift.** The stored PDF must match the record served by the API. Added to Task 2.12: `"REQ-039 the document is never re-rendered after issue"`. A status change to Paid leaves `document.sha256` and the stored bytes byte-identical.
5. **E-mail before commit.** AC-11 can consume `InvoiceIssued` only after the outbox commit, but the document fetch could race a replica that has not hydrated yet. Added to Task 2.15: `"redelivers when the document is not yet readable"`. The test exists; the listener must throw, not ack.

#### Dependencies
**Needs from other workstreams:**
- **WS1 (settlement, two-level model):** keeps `PairSettlementRecord` and the pair loop in `settlement.service.ts:339-376`. My Tasks 2.6 and 2.7 edit that loop: rebase whichever lands second. WS1 builds the monthly statement (REQ-059) from `InvoiceIssued.amounts` (Task 2.2) or from `GET /internal/v1/invoices`, and removes `settlementType: "early"` producers.
- **WS3 (payments, KYC, payout):** "AC-07 pays by PaymentIntent direct charge on the producer's connected account with `metadata.invoiceId`, reading `GetInvoice` v2 (`amounts.total`, `legalNumber`, no `stripeInvoiceId`)". This gates Task 2.13. WS3 also removes `invoice.paid` and `invoice.payment_failed` from `PAYMENT_WEBHOOK_EVENTS`. WS3 or the T&C owner stores the mandate `acceptedAt` (REQ-050) in AC-03; until then it is `null` on the PDF line.
- **WS4 (EWDS adapters):**
  - provides an `EwdsTariffSource` implementation bound to `EWDS_TARIFF_SOURCE` in AC-13 (`TARIFF_PROVIDER=ewds`);
  - routes the command `sendReminder` to `InvoicingService.sendReminder(invoiceId, {actorId, communityId, trigger:"manual"})`;
  - maps `adjustedPrice` from the GSY settlement output into `TradeFixture.adjustedPricePerKWhEur` / `Trade.adjustedPrice`;
  - owns the `billingInvoice` topic version bump, which must include my `gridFeeAmount` change (Task 2.1 edits the same schema file).
- **Live-mode workstream (A10.1):** the real `billingMode` on the outbox envelope (Task 2.11 keeps `"virtual"` as the default).

**Provides:**
- The `TariffPort`, `Tariff`, `EwdsTariffSource`, `EWDS_TARIFF_SOURCE` and `createTariffPort` in AC-13, and `GET /internal/v1/communities/{id}/tariff`.
- `ReferenceDataPort.getTariff` and `computeTariffCharges` in AC-05.

## Workstream 3 — KYC for all, participants data, live mode, GDPR completeness

Every participant (consumer, producer, prosumer) gets a Stripe Connect account with Stripe-hosted or embedded onboarding (KYC). Community managers do Stripe Identity verification only. Billing owns `kycVerified`, logs each KYC change and publishes `KycStatusChanged`. That flag gates statements, invoices, payments and payouts (A5, REQ-045, REQ-051).
Participants arrive from the EWDS (Actor/Address/ContactDetails) through an `ActorSourcePort`; CSV import stays as the fallback. T&C acceptance keeps the self-billing mandate for producers and stores the acceptance time (REQ-008, REQ-050, REQ-058).
Live mode becomes a per-community setting, guarded by the operator role and live Stripe keys (REQ-043, A10.1). GDPR closes: full DSAR export (Art. 15/20), real IdP/PSP erasure adapters selected by environment, an `ErasureRequestPort` for requests from the EWDS, and explicit retention periods (REQ-011, REQ-013).

**Conventions used below.** `R` = repo root (`outputs/repo`, develop @6a24cc3). Test command per module: `pnpm --filter @intelligent/<module> exec vitest run <spec path>`. Full gate: `pnpm test` (80% coverage per module). Every commit message ends with `(INT-TBD)`. Events and logs carry identifiers only. New ports follow the existing `create<X>(env)` factory pattern (`services/participants/src/idp/idp-admin.factory.ts`): the mock is the default, and the real adapter is selected by environment.

### Task 3.4: KycService — one readiness rule for all roles, `KycStatusChanged`

**Files:** create `services/payments/src/kyc/kyc.service.ts` and `.spec.ts`. Modify `payments.service.ts`:

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `export function kycReady(a: AccountRecord): boolean`. Rule: `a.kycVerified && (a.role === "producer" || Boolean(a.paymentMethodId))`. Every role needs KYC; buyers (consumer, prosumer) also need a payment method.
  - `export function canReceivePayout(a: AccountRecord): boolean`. Rule: `a.kycVerified && a.payoutAccountVerified`.
  - `KycService.applyAccountSession(participantId: string, session: OnboardingSession, source: KycLogEntry["source"], pspEventId: string | null): Promise<AccountRecord>`. It sets `kycStatus`: `complete` → verified, `disabledReason` starting with `rejected.` → rejected, anything else → pending. It also sets `kycVerified`, `kycVerifiedAt` (first time only) and `payoutAccountVerified = payoutsEnabled === true`. It appends a log entry, and emits `KycStatusChanged` only when `kycStatus` or `payoutAccountVerified` changed.
- Consumes: `KycLogStore` (3.2), `OutboxService`, `BillingModeResolver` (3.13).

### Task 3.5: Single onboarding path (Connect for every participant, embedded or hosted)

**Files:** `services/payments/src/payments/payments.service.ts` (`startOnboarding` l.378-417, `startProducerOnboarding` l.419-442 renamed `startKycOnboarding`, `provisionPaymentAccount` consumer branch l.306-332); `payments.controller.ts` (l.34-45); spec.
**Interfaces:**
- Produces `startOnboarding(input: { participantId: string; communityId: string; role: ProvisionRole; surface?: "embedded" | "hosted"; returnUrl?: string }): Promise<OnboardingSession>`.
- Order for every role:
  1. Connect KYC first: `createEmbeddedProducerAccount` (renamed `createKycAccount` by the payments workstream), then an Account Session (`embedded`) or `createHostedKycLink` (`hosted`).
  2. Then a SetupIntent (`elements`) for consumer and prosumer.
- `hosted` is what the EWDS `startKyc` command uses (A7).
- `returnUrl` must match the allow-list in env `KYC_RETURN_URL_ALLOWLIST` (comma-separated origins); otherwise the call returns 400.

### Task 3.14: Live mode guard in AC-03 (remove the V1 refusal)

**Files:** `participants.service.ts` `performUpdateCommunitySettings` (l.112-125; delete l.119-122); `public.controller.ts` `putCommunitySettings` (l.77-89: roles `["manager","operator"]`, pass `req.user.roles`); create `ports/psp-capabilities.port.ts` (HTTP `GET ${PAYMENTS_INTERNAL_URL}/internal/v1/psp/capabilities`; `NoLivePspCapabilities` when unset); spec.

- `updateCommunitySettings(communityId: string, write: Omit<CommunitySettings,"communityId">, callerRoles: string[]): Promise<CommunitySettings>`.
- Rules, applied only when `billingMode` changes:
  - virtual → live needs the `operator` role (403 otherwise) and `liveConfigured === true` (409 `live_keys_missing`).

  - Any other field stays editable by the manager.
- Emits `AuditableActionPerformed {action:"community.billing-mode.live"}`.

**Covers:** REQ-043, UAT-054, A10.1.

```ts
  readiness: ReadinessStatus; kycVerified: boolean };
type PaymentAccountPersonalData = { participantId: string; pspAccountRef: string|null; paymentMethodPresent: boolean;
  kycStatus: KycStatus; kycVerified: boolean; kycVerifiedAt: string|null; payoutAccountVerified: boolean; kycLog: KycLogEntry[] };
ParticipantsService.eraseIdentity(participantId: string): Promise<{ erased: boolean }>   // Keycloak deleteUser, 404 = erased
PaymentsService.eraseAtPsp(participantId: string): Promise<{ outcome: "erased"|"retained_by_psp"|"nothing_to_erase"; reason?: "aml_legal_obligation" }>
```
- The Stripe erasure does three things:
  1. `customers.del` for every platform and connected Customer;
  2. `accounts.del` in test mode;
  3. in live mode, no account deletion and the outcome `retained_by_psp` with reason `aml_legal_obligation`, because Stripe keeps KYC data as an independent controller under AML law (document this in D5.5).

**Covers:** REQ-013, UAT-013.

  - `it("REQ-013 / UAT-013: test-mode erase deletes customers and the connected account")`: Stripe stub; expect `customers.del` called 2× and `accounts.del("acct_1")`.

### Task 3.22: `ErasureRequestPort` — erasure requests from the EWDS

**Files:** create `services/compliance/src/erasure/erasure-request.port.ts`, `mock-erasure-request.adapter.ts`, `erasure-request.listener.ts` and specs. Modify `compliance.service.ts` (new `acceptExternalErasureRequest`), `domain/types.ts` (`DataSubjectRequest.origin`, `externalRequestId?`) and `app.module.ts`.
**Interfaces (produces):**
```ts
export type InboundErasureRequest = { externalRequestId: string; actorId: string; communityId: string; issuedBy: string; receivedAt: string };
export interface ErasureRequestPort {
  onRequest(handler: (r: InboundErasureRequest) => Promise<void>): void;
  reportOutcome(externalRequestId: string, outcome: { status: "completed"|"failed"; requestId: string; invoicesRetainedUntil: string|null }): Promise<void>;
}
export const ERASURE_REQUEST_PORT = Symbol("ERASURE_REQUEST_PORT");
export function createErasureRequestPort(env?): ErasureRequestPort; // ERASURE_REQUEST_SOURCE=mock|ewds|none (default none); ewds = WS4
ComplianceService.acceptExternalErasureRequest(r: InboundErasureRequest): Promise<{ requestId: string; duplicate: boolean }>
```
- Handling is idempotent on `externalRequestId`.
- `actorId` must resolve to a participant of `communityId` (`ParticipantCommunityPort.communityOf`); otherwise the outcome is reported as `failed` with no erasure.
- `origin = "ewds"`, `actorId = "ewds:" + issuedBy`.
- `maybeCompleteErasure` (l.421-448) calls `reportOutcome` for `origin === "ewds"`.

**Covers:** REQ-013 (request received from the EWDS), UAT-013.

#### Review focus (Workstream 3)

1. **Mode switch with an open period.** A community switched to live mid-month mixes test-mode and live-mode invoices for the same period.
   - Test added to 3.14: `it("REQ-043: switch to live refused while settlement has an unbilled or open period")`. It consumes `GET /internal/v1/communities/{id}/open-periods` from the settlement workstream; until that exists, a stub returning `["2026-11"]` gives 409 `open_period`.
2. **`account.updated` arriving before the account row is stored**, during the first embedded onboarding of a consumer. The webhook for an unknown `participantId` is dropped and KYC stays `pending`.
   - Test added to 3.4: `it("REQ-051: account.updated for unknown participant is retried (throws), not dropped")`. Stripe then redelivers.
3. **Fail-open KYC client.** `AllowAllKycStatusClient` could reach a cluster through a missing `PAYMENTS_INTERNAL_URL`.
   - Test added to 3.8: `it("REQ-045: AllowAll client refused when NODE_ENV=production")`. The factory throws.
4. **EWDS actor update overwrites a profile the participant corrected in the portal.**
   - Test added to 3.11: `it("REQ-008: EWDS update wins and emits ParticipantProfileReplaced audit (ids only)")`. This documents that the EWDS (the controller's data) is the master once the source is `ewds`, and `PUT /me/profile` returns 409 `profile_managed_by_ewds`.
5. **Erasure race.** `ErasureRequested` reaches AC-03 (which nulls `idpUserId`) before AC-12's synchronous `eraseIdentity` call.
   - Test added to 3.21: `it("REQ-013: eraseIdentity is called before ErasureRequested is appended")`. It asserts the call order on spies.

## Workstream 4 — EWDS integration: Client Gateway adapters, env selection, topics, project-UI command channel

Build one shared DDHub Client Gateway (CGW) client (`@intelligent/ewds-client`: REST + `x-api-key`, send and receive by channel/topic, request/reply correlation, durable inbound store with a purge at 7 days or less) and real EWDS adapters for each port that is mocked today. Each adapter is selected by an env var, and **mock stays the default** (A8).
Revise the EWDS topic schemas for the two-level model: `billingStatement`, `billingPayout` and `billingKycStatus` are new; `billingInvoice` and `billingPayment` are revised. Add the **billing command channel** (request/reply on EWDS) so the project UI never calls Blooo directly (A7). The command channel authorizes each request on three things: the caller's gateway DID, the `actorId` in the payload, and a role check against the participants registry. Replies carry only Stripe-hosted URLs.
`/v1` is documented as the API of `apps/web` (the reference client) only. The GSY off-chain REST route becomes a test fixture only. CI validates every topic schema with positive and negative fixtures.

Evidence: `gap/G1_integration.md` B-01, B-02, B-03 and B-20. Code facts this plan relies on:
- `services/trade-intake/src/intake/intake.service.ts:28-29` hard-wires `createMockSuite("S1")`.
- `services/trade-intake/src/app.module.ts:62-81` defaults `INTAKE_BOOTSTRAP_S1` to `"true"`, and `deploy/charts/trade-intake/values.yaml:30` also sets it to `"true"`.
- `services/publication/src/app.module.ts:21` binds `useClass: MockDataspaceAdapter`.
- `MarketPort` and `DataSpacePort` (`mocks/src/types.ts:93-107`) are **synchronous**.
- The schemas are copied twice: `docs/architecture/ewds-topics/` and `mocks/schemas/`.

GSY channel facts come from the brain note "Source - GSY DEX v2 repository":
- Channels: `gsy.intelligent.requests.*` and `gsy.intelligent.responses.*`, owner `integration.apps.intelligent.auth.ewc`, version `1.0.0`.
- Request envelope `{requestId, operation, payload}`; response envelope `{requestId, success, data[], error}`.
- `tradesQuery` windows are at most 86 400 s.
- Ids are bytes16 hex.
- Timestamps come as epoch integers or RFC 3339 strings (the schema and the runtime disagree).

### Task 4.1: Contract — revised and new EWDS record topic schemas

**Files:** `docs/architecture/ewds-topics/*.schema.json`, `*.example.json`, `fixtures/invalid/*.json`, `README.md`; `scripts/validate-ewds-schemas.mjs` (l.17 `names`), `scripts/validate-ewds-schemas.test.mjs`
**Interfaces:** Produces `validateEwdsSchemas(): Promise<Array<{name: string; ok: boolean; errors: string[]}>>` (now 7 names) and `validateInvalidFixtures(): Promise<Array<{file: string; rejected: boolean}>>`.
**Covers:** REQ-048, REQ-059, REQ-054, REQ-004, REQ-015, REQ-060, NFR-004, UAT-068

Schema decisions. The files stay at `v1.0.0` because none is registered with EWAG yet; the README moves to draft v0.2. All files use JSON Schema 2020-12 with `additionalProperties: false`.
- **`billingStatement`** (new; ontology `Billing`). It carries no PII.
  - Required: `statementId` (uuid, IRI `int:billingId`), `communityId`, `actorId` (participant), `billingMode`, `billingPeriodFrom`, `billingPeriodUntil`, `statementStatus` (enum Open|PartiallyPaid|Paid|Overdue|Void|Disputed, from ontology `billingStatus`), `totalBought`, `totalSold` (≥0), `netBalance` (signed number), `balanceDirection` (enum `pay|receive|zero`), `totalEnergyAmount`, `totalGridFeeAmount`, `totalTaxAmount`, `totalAmountDue`, `totalAmountPaid`, `outstandingAmount`, `currency`, `invoiceIds` (uuid[]), `createdAt`, `updatedAt`.
  - Optional: `payoutIds` (uuid[]|null).
- **`billingInvoice`** (revised). The pair model and `selfBilling` stay (REQ-054).
  - `gridFeeAmount` loses `maximum: 0`.
  - New `gridFeeStatus` enum `applied|not_published` (A10.2 / REQ-004 wording).
  - `settlementType` enum becomes `periodic|final`. `early` is removed (REQ-057 stays deprecated).
  - `billingId` keeps its ontology meaning: it is the `statementId` of the **recipient** (consumer).
  - New required `issuerStatementId` (uuid): the producer's statement. A pair invoice appears in two statements; this is how "add statementId" is implemented without breaking the ontology FK.
  - `billingMode` description drops "all communities virtual".
- **`billingPayment`** (revised):
  - New optional `initiatedAt` and `settledAt` (date-time|null), for the KPI (ER1.4).
  - `payeeId` description becomes "producer Actor; funds go to the producer's connected account (direct charge, REQ-014)".
  - `stripeIds` stays out.
- **`billingPayout`** (new):
  - Required: `payoutId`, `actorId`, `communityId`, `billingMode`, `amount` (>0), `currency`, `payoutStatus` (enum `requested|pending|in_transit|paid|failed|cancelled`), `trigger` (`manual|auto`), `createdAt`, `updatedAt`.
  - Optional: `statementIds` (uuid[]), `arrivalDate` (date|null), `failureCategory` (enum `account_closed|invalid_account|insufficient_balance|provider_error|other|null`).
  - No IBAN and no bank name.
- **`billingKycStatus`** (new; ontology `UserAccount` subset):
  - Required: `actorId`, `communityId`, `kycVerified` (boolean), `accountStatus` (enum PendingVerification|Active|Suspended|Locked|Deactivated), `verificationKind` (`connect_account|identity`), `updatedAt`.
  - No document data and no name.

### Task 4.2: Contract — command channel schemas and AsyncAPI

**Files:** `docs/architecture/ewds-topics/billingCommandRequest.v1.0.0.schema.json`, `billingCommandReply.v1.0.0.schema.json` + examples; `contracts/asyncapi/ewds-billing.v1.yaml`
**Interfaces:** Produces the wire envelopes, which mirror the GSY convention:
- `BillingCommandRequest = { requestId: uuid; operation: "startPayment"|"requestWithdrawal"|"setInvoiceStatus"|"startKyc"|"sendReminder"|"triggerBillingRun"; actorId: uuid; payload: object }`. `oneOf` keyed on `operation`, one payload per operation:
  - `startPayment {invoiceId, returnUrl}`
  - `requestWithdrawal {currency}`
  - `setInvoiceStatus {invoiceId, status, reason(minLength 3)}`
  - `startKyc {returnUrl, refreshUrl}`
  - `sendReminder {invoiceId}`
  - `triggerBillingRun {communityId, period: "^\\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2]|Q[1-4])$", type: "periodic"|"final"}`
- `BillingCommandReply = { requestId; operation; success: boolean; data?: object; error?: {code: "forbidden"|"not_found"|"invalid"|"conflict"|"unavailable"; message: string} }`.
  - A redirect reply has `data.redirect = {provider:"stripe", purpose:"checkout"|"account_onboarding"|"identity_verification", url, expiresAt}`.
  - `url` has pattern `^https://(checkout|connect|verify)\\.stripe\\.com/`.

The AsyncAPI channels are:
- `blooo.intelligent.billing.pub`: send `billingStatement`, `billingInvoice`, `billingPayment`, `billingPayout`, `billingKycStatus`, with `$ref` to the topic JSON files.
- `blooo.intelligent.billing.commands.sub`: receive `billingCommandRequest`.
- `blooo.intelligent.billing.replies.pub`: send `billingCommandReply`, correlated by CGW `transactionId` = `requestId`.

**Covers:** REQ-052 (v6.1 wording per A7), REQ-048, REQ-044, REQ-061, REQ-006, REQ-051

### Task 4.3: Contract — internal operations called by the command channel

**Files:**
- `contracts/openapi/internal/payments.v1.yaml`
- `invoicing.v1.yaml`
- `notification.v1.yaml`
- `participants.v1.yaml`

**Interfaces:** These are the internal routes, each with a service bearer token (Keycloak client credentials, `resolveServiceAuthHeaders("ac-10-publication")`). `startRun`, `setInvoiceStatus` and `startOnboarding` exist today; the other service methods are named per the WS1-WS3 brief and are listed under Dependencies.

| Command | Internal route (new unless noted) | Service method |
|---|---|---|
| startPayment | `POST /internal/v1/payments:start` `{invoiceId, actorId, surface, returnUrl?}` → `{paymentId, status, handoff?}` (A7-bis) | `PaymentsService.startPayment` (WS1; replaces `payInvoice` for hosted Checkout) |
| requestWithdrawal | `POST /internal/v1/payouts:request` `{actorId, currency}` → `{payoutId, payoutStatus, amount, currency}` | `PaymentsService.requestWithdrawal` (WS1) |
| startKyc | `POST /internal/v1/kyc:start` `{actorId, kind: "connect_account"\|"identity", surface, returnUrl?, refreshUrl?}` → `PspHandoff` (A7-bis) | `PaymentsService.startKyc` (WS1/WS3; today's `startOnboarding` returns an embedded `clientSecret`, which is not a URL) |
| setInvoiceStatus | `POST /internal/v1/invoices/{invoiceId}/status` `{status, reason, actorId, communityId}` → public invoice | `InvoicingService.setInvoiceStatus(invoiceId, {status, reason, actorId, communityId})` (**exists**, `invoicing.service.ts:425`) |
| sendReminder | `POST /internal/v1/reminders` `{invoiceId, actorId, communityId}` → `{invoiceId, sentAt}` | `NotificationService.sendReminder` (WS2/WS3, REQ-061) |
| triggerBillingRun | `POST /internal/v1/billing-runs` (**exists**, `settlement.controller.ts:36`) `{communityId, type, period}` → `{runId}` | `SettlementService.startRun` (**exists**, `settlement.service.ts:264`) |
| (authz) | `GET /internal/v1/actors/{actorId}/authorization` → `ActorAuthorization` | `ActorAuthorizationService.authorize` (this WS, Task 4.14) |
| (authz) | `PUT /internal/v1/communities/{communityId}/managers/{actorId}` (operator) | `ActorAuthorizationService.bindManager` (this WS) |

`ActorAuthorization = { actorId: string; status: "active"|"offboarded"|"unknown"; memberships: Array<{communityId: string; role: "member"|"manager"|"operator"; participantRole?: "consumer"|"producer"|"prosumer"}> }`

```ts
export type CgwConfig = { baseUrl: string; apiKey: string; clientId: string; fetchImpl?: typeof fetch; timeoutMs?: number };
export type CgwSend = { fqcn: string; topicName: string; topicVersion: string; topicOwner: string; transactionId?: string; payload: unknown };
export type CgwMessage = { id: string; topicName: string; topicOwner: string; topicVersion: string; transactionId: string | null; payload: string; sender: string; signatureValid: boolean; timestampNanos: number };
export class CgwClient {
  constructor(cfg: CgwConfig);
  send(m: CgwSend): Promise<{ messageId: string; sent: number; failed: number }>;           // POST /api/v2/messages
  receive(q: { fqcn: string; topicName?: string; topicOwner?: string; amount?: number }): Promise<CgwMessage[]>; // GET /api/v2/messages?clientId=
  ack(messageIds: string[]): Promise<void>;                                                    // POST /api/v2/messages/ack
  identity(): Promise<{ did: string; balance: string }>;                                       // GET /api/v2/identity (health, REQ-029)
}
export class CgwError extends Error { constructor(readonly status: number, readonly path: string); }
```

### Task 4.6: `@intelligent/ewds-client` — topic consumer, durable inbound store, purge ≤ 7 days, schema validator

**Interfaces:** Produces:
```ts
export type InboundRecord = { messageId: string; topicName: string; sender: string; receivedAt: string; payload: string | null; status: "received" | "processed" | "rejected" };
export interface InboundMessageStore { saveIfNew(r: InboundRecord): Promise<boolean>; mark(messageId: string, status: "processed" | "rejected"): Promise<void>; purge(now: Date, maxAgeMs?: number): Promise<number>; }
export const MAX_RETENTION_MS = 7 * 24 * 3600 * 1000;
export class EwdsTopicConsumer<T> {
  constructor(cgw: CgwClient, store: InboundMessageStore, sub: { fqcn: string; topicName: string; topicOwner: string; allowedSenders?: string[] }, validate?: (x: unknown) => x is T);
  pull(max?: number): Promise<Array<{ messageId: string; sender: string; body: T }>>;   // persist-then-ack; dedupe on messageId
  done(messageId: string, ok: boolean): Promise<void>;                                 // status processed|rejected; payload set to null
}
export function createTopicValidator(topic: EwdsTopicName): (x: unknown) => { ok: boolean; errors: string[] };
export type EwdsTopicName = "billingStatement"|"billingInvoice"|"billingPayment"|"billingPayout"|"billingKycStatus"|"billingCommandRequest"|"billingCommandReply";
```

  ```ts
    await expect(s.purge(new Date(), 8 * 86400000)).rejects.toThrow("retention above 7 days");
  ```

- `publish` validates with `createTopicValidator(topic)` first, so an invalid message is never sent. It then calls `cgw.send({ fqcn: "blooo.intelligent.billing.pub", topicName: topic, transactionId: <aggregateId>, payload })`.

### Task 4.15: publication — command authorizer

**Files:** `services/publication/src/commands/command-authorizer.ts` (+spec), `src/commands/participants-authz.client.ts`
**Interfaces:**
- Consumes `GET /internal/v1/actors/{actorId}/authorization` and `GET /internal/v1/invoices/{invoiceId}` (invoicing, exists) to get `communityId`, `recipientId` and `issuerId`.
- Produces:
```ts
type AuthzDecision = { allow: true; communityId: string; role: "member"|"manager"|"operator" } | { allow: false; code: "forbidden"|"not_found" };
class CommandAuthorizer {
  constructor(allowedDids: string[], authz: ActorAuthzClient, invoices: InvoiceLookupClient);
  decide(senderDid: string, signatureValid: boolean, req: BillingCommandRequest): Promise<AuthzDecision>;
}
```

Policy. Layer 1 is the CGW channel restriction on `blooo.intelligent.billing.commands.sub` (UI gateway DID or project role, configured with EWAG). Layer 2 requires `senderDid ∈ EWDS_COMMAND_ALLOWED_DIDS` **and** `signatureValid`. Layer 3 checks the `actorId` role per command:

| Command | Role check |
|---|---|
| startPayment | member, and actor = the invoice's `invoiceIssuedTo` |
| requestWithdrawal | member with participantRole producer or prosumer |
| setInvoiceStatus | manager of the invoice's community |
| startKyc | member gets `connect_account`; manager gets `identity` |
| sendReminder | manager of the invoice's community |
| triggerBillingRun | manager of `payload.communityId`, or operator |

**Covers:** REQ-052 (A7), REQ-049, REQ-044, NFR-015
- [ ] **Write failing test:**
  ```ts
  it("A7: unknown sender DID -> forbidden, no internal call", async () => { expect(await a.decide("did:ethr:0xEVIL", true, req)).toEqual({ allow: false, code: "forbidden" }); expect(authz.calls).toBe(0); });
  it("A7: signatureValid false -> forbidden", ...);
  it("REQ-049: manager of C2 cannot setInvoiceStatus on invoice of C1", ...);              // forbidden
  it("REQ-014: member cannot startPayment on an invoice issued to someone else", ...);      // forbidden
  it("consumer-only member requestWithdrawal -> forbidden", ...);
  it("operator triggerBillingRun on any community -> allow role operator", ...);
  ```
- [ ] Run `pnpm --filter @intelligent/publication exec vitest run src/commands/command-authorizer.spec.ts`. Expected FAIL.

**Files:** `services/publication/src/commands/command-dispatcher.ts` (+spec), `src/commands/command-targets.client.ts` (+spec), `src/commands/processed-commands.store.ts`
**Interfaces:**

```ts
interface CommandTargetsPort {
  startPayment(i: { invoiceId: string; actorId: string; returnUrl: string }): Promise<{ paymentId: string; checkoutUrl: string; expiresAt: string }>;
  requestWithdrawal(i: { actorId: string; currency: string }): Promise<{ payoutId: string; payoutStatus: string; amount: number; currency: string }>;
  startKyc(i: { actorId: string; kind: "connect_account" | "identity"; returnUrl: string; refreshUrl: string }): Promise<{ kind: string; url: string; expiresAt: string }>;
  setInvoiceStatus(i: { invoiceId: string; status: string; reason: string; actorId: string; communityId: string }): Promise<{ invoiceId: string; invoiceStatus: string }>;
  sendReminder(i: { invoiceId: string; actorId: string; communityId: string }): Promise<{ invoiceId: string; sentAt: string }>;
  startRun(i: { communityId: string; type: "periodic" | "final"; period: string }): Promise<{ runId: string }>;
}
class CommandDispatcher {
  constructor(authz: CommandAuthorizer, targets: CommandTargetsPort, processed: ProcessedCommandsStore, returnUrlPrefixes: string[], outbox: OutboxService);
  handle(msg: { messageId: string; sender: string; signatureValid: boolean; body: BillingCommandRequest }): Promise<BillingCommandReply | null>;  // null = drop (layer-2 failure: no reply to unknown DIDs)
}
```

#### Review focus (Workstream 4)

1. **The GSY wire format is unverified** (epoch vs RFC 3339 timestamps, value scaling, error codes, possible paging of `data[]` within the 6 MB limit). The fixtures in Task 4.9 are hand-built. Added test (Task 4.9): `"rejects a response whose trade lacks market_id/energy/price with EwdsRemoteError('schema', …) instead of ingesting zeros"`. When A12 closes, replace the fixtures with recorded messages; the commit message must cite the recording date.
2. **Replicas and the CGW cursor.** With two pods on the same `clientId`, one pod would consume the other's GSY responses or commands. Added test (Task 4.7): `"two HOSTNAMEs give two clientIds"`. Added test (Task 4.17): `"a reply for a requestId not in processed and not received by this pod is not re-sent"`. Dev keeps `replicaCount: 1` for publication and trade-intake.
3. **Delegated trust in the UI gateway.** Billing trusts the `actorId` asserted by the DID-signed UI gateway, so a compromised project-UI gateway can act for any actor. Added test (Task 4.16): `"per-sender rate limit: more than 30 commands/min from one DID -> error unavailable and an operator alert event"`. Mitigations already planned: audit with `senderDid`; short-lived Stripe URLs; Checkout bound to the invoice and connected account by WS1.
4. **Stripe URLs are bearer links on the reply channel.** Added test (Task 4.17): `"reply channel fqcn is configured with payloadEncryption: true (asserted from EWDS_REPLY_ENCRYPTED=true env guard; poller refuses to start otherwise in ewds mode)"`. Ask EWAG for payload encryption on `billing.replies`.
5. **Schema drift between the 3 places a schema appears** (docs, `packages/ewds-client/schemas`, and the AsyncAPI `$ref`). Added test (Task 4.6): byte-equality of docs vs package. Added test (Task 4.2): `"asyncapi ewds-billing $refs resolve to the 7 docs schema files"`.

#### Dependencies

**Needs from WS1 (payments):**
- `PaymentsService.startPayment({invoiceId, actorId, returnUrl}) → {paymentId, checkoutUrl, expiresAt}`: a Checkout Session with a direct charge on the producer's account.
- `PaymentsService.requestWithdrawal({actorId, currency}) → {payoutId, payoutStatus, amount, currency}`.
- `PaymentsService.startKyc({actorId, kind, returnUrl, refreshUrl}) → {kind, url, expiresAt}` (Account Link or Identity VerificationSession). Today only `startOnboarding` exists, returning an embedded `clientSecret`.
- Events `PayoutStatusChanged` (`{payoutId, actorId, amount, currency, status, trigger, statementIds?, arrivalDate?, failureCategory?}`) and `KycStatusChanged` (`{actorId, kycVerified, accountStatus, verificationKind}`).
- Payment events gain `initiatedAt` and `settledAt`.
- WS4 adds the internal route wrappers; WS1 owns the method bodies.

**Needs from WS2 (settlement/invoicing):**
- Events `StatementIssued` and `StatementStatusChanged`, carrying the `billingStatement` fields from Task 4.1.
- `InvoiceIssued.data` gains `gridFee`, `gridFeeStatus`, `recipientStatementId`, `issuerStatementId`; `settlementType ∈ {periodic, final}`.
- The `TariffPort` declaration (assumed `services/reference-data/src/tariff/tariff.port.ts`, `getTariff(communityId, at)`).
- `NotificationService.sendReminder({invoiceId, actorId, communityId}) → {invoiceId, sentAt}` (REQ-061).
- `SettlementService.startRun` and `InvoicingService.setInvoiceStatus` are used as they exist.
- `TradeFixture.adjustedPricePerKWhEur` must be consumed by settlement (A10.3).

**Needs from WS3 (identity/GDPR):**
- `ActorSourcePort` (participants) and `ErasureRequestPort` (compliance) declarations and their scheduler jobs.
- The real IdP/PSP erasure adapters that run after an EWDS erasure request.

**Needs from external parties:**
- EWAG: Configured + Verified status, the `topiccreator` role, the 3 billing channels with role restrictions and payload encryption on replies, and the GSY subscriber role (A12).
- R2M: the UI gateway DID and the return-URL origin.
- GSY: recorded responses.

**Provides:**
- `@intelligent/ewds-client`: `CgwClient`, `RequestReplyClient`, `EwdsTopicConsumer<T>`, `InboundMessageStore`, `createTopicValidator`, `adapterMode` and `loadCgwConfig`.
- Ports and adapters: `MARKET_PORT` / `EwdsMarketAdapter`, `EwdsDataspaceAdapter`, `EwdsActorSourceAdapter`, `EwdsErasureRequestAdapter`, `EwdsTariffAdapter`.
- `GET /internal/v1/actors/{actorId}/authorization`.
- The 7 EWDS topic schemas and `contracts/asyncapi/ewds-billing.v1.yaml`.
- The command channel (6 operations) for the project UI.
> **Amended by A7-bis (master plan):** payment, payment-method setup and KYC sessions support both `hosted` and `embedded` surfaces, chosen per request; see the canonical interfaces in the master plan.

## Workstream 5 — PSP-neutral port and KPI measurement

Replace the Stripe-shaped `PspPort` (`services/payments/src/psp/psp.port.ts`) with four PSP-neutral ports (`KycOnboardingPort`, `CollectPort`, `PayoutPort`, `PspEventsPort`). Both adapters (mock, Stripe) must pass one shared contract suite. Card and SEPA Direct Debit stay; the `cards-only-v1` branch is abandoned (A10.5).
Measure KPI TE-2 / ER1.4 correctly: `initiatedAt` at submission, `settledAt` from the PSP webhook, `processingTimeMs = settledAt - initiatedAt`, PSP fee from the balance transaction. Add `GET /reports/payment-methods?communityId=&period=`.
Extend the KPI CSV with per-participant rows that match the WS1 monthly statement (REQ-047, UAT-058). Put the UE-3 corrective action into the failure e-mail (REQ-046, UAT-057).

Ground rules for this workstream:
- No Stripe identifiers in domain types, events, contracts or stores. "Stripe" appears only in `stripe-*.ts` files and `stripe-env.ts`.
- Money stays a decimal string with 2 decimals (BR-08). Fee arithmetic uses integer minor units.
- The platform never holds funds (A1). Charges are **direct charges on the producer's connected account** (REQ-014). Payouts go **from the participant's connected account** to their bank (REQ-015).
- V1 journals are reset (A10.6). Stores rename their columns in place. There is no data migration.
