### Task 5.1: Contracts — PSP-neutral names, KPI fields, per-method report

**Files:** `contracts/asyncapi/payments.v1.yaml` (L78-130); `contracts/openapi/public.v1.yaml` (L133-218 `/me/payment-account*`, L336-357 `/invoices/{id}/payments`, L704-728 after `/reports/summary`, L773-805 `/kpi-exports`, L1003-1060 `MePaymentAccount`, L1256-1275 `Payment`); `contracts/openapi/internal/payments.v1.yaml` (L1-201); `scripts/psp-neutral-contracts.test.mjs` (create); `scripts/run-tests.mjs` (after L60); `packages/public-api-client/src/schema.d.ts` (regenerate).
**Interfaces:** Produces these schemas:
- `PaymentStatusChanged.data` = `{ paymentId, invoiceId, payerId, payeeId, status, amount: Money, currency, method?: "card"|"sepa_debit", initiatedAt: date-time, settledAt?: date-time, processingTimeMs?: integer, feeAmount?: Money, feeCurrency?: Currency, failureCategory?: PspFailureCategory }`.
- `PspFailureCategory` enum = `card_declined | insufficient_funds | authentication_required | mandate_invalid | bank_account_invalid | provider_error | other`.
- `Payment` adds `checkoutUrl?` (uri), `initiatedAt?`, `settledAt?`, `processingTimeMs?`, `feeAmount?`, `feeCurrency?`.
- `MePaymentAccount.pspAccountRef`.
- `GET /reports/payment-methods` returns `PaymentMethodReport { communityId, period, rows: PaymentMethodStats[] }`, where `PaymentMethodStats { method, feeCurrency, count, succeededCount, failedCount, pendingCount, successRate: number|null, medianProcessingTimeMs: integer|null, totalFee: Money, averageFee: Money|null, feeMissingCount }`.

### Task 5.2: Neutral port types and the shared contract suite

**Files:** create `services/payments/src/psp/psp.types.ts`, `kyc-onboarding.port.ts`, `collect.port.ts`, `payout.port.ts`, `psp-events.port.ts`, `kyc-test-fixture.port.ts`, `testing/psp-contract.suite.ts`, `mock-psp.contract.spec.ts`.
**Interfaces (Produces, exact):**
```ts
// psp.types.ts
export type PspPaymentMethodType = "card" | "sepa_debit";
export type PspFailureCategory = "card_declined" | "insufficient_funds" | "authentication_required"
  | "mandate_invalid" | "bank_account_invalid" | "provider_error" | "other";
export type PspErrorCode = "insufficient_balance" | "account_not_ready" | "invalid_request" | "provider_unavailable";
export class PspError extends Error { constructor(readonly code: PspErrorCode, message: string) }
export type PspEventKind = "account.updated" | "checkout.completed" | "payment.processing" | "payment.succeeded"
  | "payment.failed" | "payout.paid" | "payout.failed" | "payout.canceled" | "ignored";
export type PspEvent = {
  eventId: string; occurredAt: string /* ISO, PSP event time */; kind: PspEventKind;
  pspAccountRef?: string; participantId?: string; invoiceId?: string;
  pspPaymentRef?: string; pspCheckoutRef?: string; pspPayoutRef?: string;
  method?: PspPaymentMethodType; failureCategory?: PspFailureCategory;
};
// kyc-onboarding.port.ts
export type PspAccountStatus = { pspAccountRef: string; status: "requirements_due" | "pending_verification" | "complete";
  currentlyDue: string[]; chargesEnabled: boolean; payoutsEnabled: boolean };
export type OnboardingSession = { pspAccountRef: string; mode: "hosted" | "embedded";
  onboardingUrl?: string /* hosted, PSP domain */; clientSecret?: string /* embedded, never logged */; expiresAt: string };
export interface KycOnboardingPort {
  createConnectedAccount(input: { participantId: string; country: string }): Promise<{ pspAccountRef: string }>;
  createOnboardingSession(input: { pspAccountRef: string; mode: "hosted" | "embedded"; returnUrl?: string; refreshUrl?: string }): Promise<OnboardingSession>;
  getAccountStatus(pspAccountRef: string): Promise<PspAccountStatus>;
}
export const KYC_ONBOARDING_PORT = Symbol("KYC_ONBOARDING_PORT");
// collect.port.ts
export type PaymentMethodDetails = { pspPaymentMethodRef: string; type?: PspPaymentMethodType; brand?: string; last4?: string };
export type DirectChargeInput = { invoiceId: string; amount: string; currency: string; pspAccountRef: string;
  pspCustomerRef: string; pspPaymentMethodRef: string; idempotencyKey: string };
export type CollectResult = { pspPaymentRef: string; status: "pending" | "succeeded" | "failed";
  method?: PspPaymentMethodType; failureCategory?: PspFailureCategory };
export type CheckoutInput = { invoiceId: string; amount: string; currency: string; pspAccountRef: string;
  pspCustomerRef: string; methods: PspPaymentMethodType[]; saveForFutureUse: boolean;
  successUrl: string; cancelUrl: string; idempotencyKey: string };
export type CheckoutSession = { pspCheckoutRef: string; checkoutUrl: string; expiresAt: string };
export type ChargeSettlement = { method: PspPaymentMethodType; feeAmount: string; feeCurrency: string; fundsAvailableAt?: string };
export interface CollectPort {
  createPaymentMethodSetup(participantId: string): Promise<{ clientSecret: string }>;
  attachPaymentMethod(participantId: string, pspPaymentMethodRef: string): Promise<{ pspPaymentMethodRef: string }>;
  getPaymentMethodDetails(pspPaymentMethodRef: string): Promise<PaymentMethodDetails>;
  ensureCustomer(input: { participantId: string; pspAccountRef: string }): Promise<{ pspCustomerRef: string }>;
  createDirectCharge(input: DirectChargeInput): Promise<CollectResult>;
  createCheckoutSession(input: CheckoutInput): Promise<CheckoutSession>;
  getChargeSettlement(input: { pspPaymentRef: string; pspAccountRef: string }): Promise<ChargeSettlement | null>;
  refund(input: { pspPaymentRef: string; pspAccountRef: string }): Promise<{ status: "refunded" }>;
}
export const COLLECT_PORT = Symbol("COLLECT_PORT");
// payout.port.ts  (names agreed with WS1)
export type PayoutResult = { pspPayoutRef: string; status: "pending" | "in_transit" | "paid" | "failed" | "canceled";
  arrivalDate?: string; failureCategory?: PspFailureCategory };
export interface PayoutPort {
  setManualSchedule(accountRef: string): Promise<void>;
  getAvailableBalance(accountRef: string, currency: string): Promise<{ amount: string; currency: string }>;
  createPayout(accountRef: string, amount: string, currency: string, idempotencyKey: string): Promise<PayoutResult>;
}
export const PAYOUT_PORT = Symbol("PAYOUT_PORT");
// psp-events.port.ts
export type PspEventVerification = { ok: false } | { ok: true; event: PspEvent };
export interface PspEventsPort { verifyEvent(rawBody: string, signature: string): PspEventVerification }
export const PSP_EVENTS_PORT = Symbol("PSP_EVENTS_PORT");
export interface PayoutEventsHandler { onPayoutEvent(event: PspEvent): Promise<void> }
export const PAYOUT_EVENTS_HANDLER = Symbol("PAYOUT_EVENTS_HANDLER");
// testing/psp-contract.suite.ts
export type PspAdapter = KycOnboardingPort & CollectPort & PayoutPort & PspEventsPort;
export type PspContractHarness = {
  adapter: PspAdapter;
  completeKyc(pspAccountRef: string): Promise<void>;
  fundBalance(pspAccountRef: string, amount: string, currency: string): Promise<void>;
  paymentMethod(kind: "card_ok" | "card_decline" | "sepa_ok"): Promise<string>;
  signedEvent(e: Omit<PspEvent, "eventId" | "occurredAt"> & { occurredAt?: string }): { rawBody: string; signature: string };
};
export function runPspContractSuite(label: string, makeHarness: () => Promise<PspContractHarness>): void;
```
**Covers:** REQ-014, REQ-015, REQ-016, REQ-051, UAT-014, UAT-015, UAT-016.

```ts
  expect(r1).toMatchObject({ status: "succeeded", method: "card" }); expect(r2.pspPaymentRef).toBe(r1.pspPaymentRef);
});
it("UAT-016 REQ-016 declined card maps to card_declined", async () => {
  expect(await a.createDirectCharge({ ...input, pspPaymentMethodRef: await h.paymentMethod("card_decline"), idempotencyKey: "k2" }))
    .toMatchObject({ status: "failed", failureCategory: "card_declined" });
});
it("UAT-057 REQ-046 settlement exposes the PSP fee", async () => {
  const s = await a.getChargeSettlement({ pspPaymentRef: r1.pspPaymentRef, pspAccountRef });
  expect(s).toMatchObject({ method: "card", feeCurrency: "EUR" }); expect(s!.feeAmount).toMatch(/^\d+\.\d{2}$/);
});
it("REQ-014 hosted checkout offers card and SEPA on the producer account", async () => {
  const c = await a.createCheckoutSession({ invoiceId: INV2, amount: "42.50", currency: "EUR", pspAccountRef, pspCustomerRef,
    methods: ["card", "sepa_debit"], saveForFutureUse: true, successUrl: RET, cancelUrl: REF, idempotencyKey: `co:${INV2}` });
  expect(c.checkoutUrl).toMatch(/^https:\/\//); expect(c.pspCheckoutRef).toMatch(/\S+/);
});
it("UAT-015 REQ-015 manual schedule then idempotent payout", async () => {
  await a.setManualSchedule(pspAccountRef); await h.fundBalance(pspAccountRef, "80.00", "EUR");
  expect(await a.getAvailableBalance(pspAccountRef, "EUR")).toEqual({ amount: "80.00", currency: "EUR" });
  const p1 = await a.createPayout(pspAccountRef, "80.00", "EUR", "po:P1:2026-11");
  const p2 = await a.createPayout(pspAccountRef, "80.00", "EUR", "po:P1:2026-11");
  expect(p2.pspPayoutRef).toBe(p1.pspPayoutRef); expect(["pending", "in_transit"]).toContain(p1.status);
});
it("REQ-015 payout above the available balance is refused", async () => {
  await expect(a.createPayout(pspAccountRef, "999.00", "EUR", "po:big")).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "insufficient_balance" });
});
it("NFR-015 unsigned event rejected; signed events normalised", async () => {
  expect(a.verifyEvent("{}", "bad")).toEqual({ ok: false });
  const ok = h.signedEvent({ kind: "payment.succeeded", pspAccountRef, pspPaymentRef: "ref1", occurredAt: "2026-11-24T10:00:03.000Z" });
  expect(a.verifyEvent(ok.rawBody, ok.signature)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, event: { kind: "payment.succeeded", pspPaymentRef: "ref1", occurredAt: "2026-11-24T10:00:03.000Z" } });
  const pf = h.signedEvent({ kind: "payout.failed", pspAccountRef, pspPayoutRef: "po1", failureCategory: "bank_account_invalid" });
  expect(a.verifyEvent(pf.rawBody, pf.signature)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, event: { kind: "payout.failed", failureCategory: "bank_account_invalid" } });
});
```

---

#### Review focus (WS5)

1. **The webhook arrives before `payInvoice` has stored the row.** A card direct charge can fire `payment_intent.succeeded` before the transaction commits. The handler then finds no row and drops the event, so `settledAt` is lost for good.
   - Fix: the handler throws a retryable error (the consumer marks it failed and Stripe retries).
   - Test added in 5.8: `"TE-2 unknown pspPaymentRef is retried, not acknowledged"`, expecting `receiveWebhook` to reject and the next delivery to settle.
2. **Fee currency differs from the charge currency.** A CHF community whose producer account settles in EUR gets fees in EUR, and the report would sum mixed currencies.
   - Test added in 5.11: `"ER1.4 rows split by feeCurrency"`. Two card payments with `feeCurrency` CHF and EUR give two rows.
3. **Pay is replayed after the 24 h idempotency window.** Stripe idempotency keys expire, so a second click on a stale invoice could create a second PaymentIntent.
   - Test added in 5.7: `"REQ-014 replay after a succeeded payment returns the stored payment and never calls the PSP"` (keeps `findSucceededByInvoiceId`, old L774-797).
4. **Mock/Stripe drift in the event shape.** The mock signs neutral JSON, while Stripe normalises real events, so a field added on one side only passes the suite silently.
   - Test added in 5.2: `"NFR-015 verified events contain only PspEvent keys"`. It asserts `Object.keys(event)` ⊆ the allowed key list for both adapters.
5. **PII leaking into PSP metadata or logs.** The checkout product name or metadata could carry a name or e-mail.
   - Test added in 5.4: `"NFR-004 Stripe requests carry identifiers only"`. It scans every fake-SDK call payload for `@` and for the participant display-name fixture, and expects no match.

#### Dependencies

**Needs from other workstreams:**
- **WS1 (statement and payout).** Emits `ParticipantStatementIssued` with the `data` fields listed in Task 5.12 (rename both sides if WS1 chooses another name). Provides a `PayoutEventsHandler` bound to `PAYOUT_EVENTS_HANDLER`. Calls `PayoutPort.setManualSchedule(accountRef)` once KYC completes, and `PayoutPort.createPayout(accountRef, amount, currency, idempotencyKey)`; the idempotency key format is `po:<participantId>:<period>`.
- **Invoicing workstream (in-house PDF, A6).** The internal invoice lookup returns `producerPspAccountRef`, with no `stripeInvoiceId`. It stops calling `connected-customers:ensure` (the endpoint is kept, renamed, until then).
- **KYC workstream (A5).** Consumes `KycOnboardingPort.createConnectedAccount`, `createOnboardingSession({ mode: "hosted" })` and `getAccountStatus`. Owns the `kycVerified` gating and Stripe Identity for managers, which is not in this port.
- **EWDS command-channel workstream (A7).** `startPayment` calls `PaymentsService.payInvoice` and returns `checkoutUrl` when present.
- **GDPR workstream.** Renames `stripeCustomerId` to `pspCustomerRef` in `services/compliance/src/psp/psp-erase.port.ts`, mirroring 5.6.
- **Publication workstream.** Maps `PspFailureCategory` onto the `billingPayment.failureCategory` enum; it is identical except `bank_account_invalid`, which applies to payouts only.

**Provides:**
- `KycOnboardingPort`, `CollectPort`, `PayoutPort`, `PspEventsPort`, `PspEvent`, `PspFailureCategory`, `PspError`, and the tokens `KYC_ONBOARDING_PORT`, `COLLECT_PORT`, `PAYOUT_PORT`, `PSP_EVENTS_PORT`, `PAYOUT_EVENTS_HANDLER`.
- `runPspContractSuite` (any future Mangopay or Lemonway adapter must pass it).
- An extended `PaymentStatusChanged`.
- `GET /reports/payment-methods`, the 27-column KPI CSV, and the shared `suggestedCorrectiveAction`.
- **For the docs workstream:** the TE-2 definition text (in the Task 5.1 contract description) and the D5.5 note that test-mode fees are Stripe-simulated.

### Task 6.2: CI guard against re-introducing personal identifiers

**Files:** create `scripts/public-hygiene.mjs`, `scripts/public-hygiene.test.mjs`; modify `scripts/run-tests.mjs` (insert after l.60).
**Interfaces:** Produces
```ts
// scripts/public-hygiene.mjs
export type HygieneRule = "jira-account-id" | "personal-blooo-email" | "brain-question-id";
export function findHygieneViolations(files: { path: string; text: string }[]): { path: string; line: number; rule: HygieneRule }[];
export async function listTrackedTextFiles(root: string): Promise<string[]>; // `git ls-files`, minus pnpm-lock.yaml and binaries
```
Rules: `jira-account-id` = 24 lowercase hex chars not part of a longer hex run, or `712020:` + UUID, in `*.md|*.mdc|*.mjs|*.ts|*.yaml`; `personal-blooo-email` = `[a-z.]+@blooo\.io` except `security@blooo.io`; `brain-question-id` = `(?<![A-Z])Q-\d{3}\b` in `docs/**`. No personal names in the rule set (a names list would publish them).
**Covers:** A10.7.

### Task 6.6: Spec sync script (vault → repo) that keeps run evidence

**Files:** create `scripts/sync-spec.mjs`, `scripts/sync-spec.test.mjs`; wire in `scripts/run-tests.mjs`.
**Interfaces:** Produces
```ts
export const RUN_FIELDS: readonly ["status", "last_run", "tester", "run_path"];
export function splitNote(text: string): { fm: Record<string, unknown>; spec: string; runNotes: string[] }; // runNotes = bullet/paragraph lines under "## Run notes"
export function mergeUatNote(vaultText: string, repoText: string | null, today: string): { text: string; rerun: boolean };
export function findConfidentialMarkers(text: string): string[]; // Q-\d{3}, "project-context", "brain/", any e-mail not @example.invalid
export async function syncSpec(o: { from: string; to: string; today: string; dryRun: boolean }): Promise<{ copied: string[]; rerun: string[]; blocked: { file: string; markers: string[] }[] }>;
// CLI: node scripts/sync-spec.mjs --from "$VAULT" [--dry-run]  → prints JSON; exit 2 when blocked.length > 0
```
Merge rules (one line each): spec fields and the Given/When/Then body come from the vault; `RUN_FIELDS` come from the repo; `rerun = true` when `requirement|given|when|then|scope_d55` differ (then `status: "Not Run"`, `last_run: null`, `tester: null`, keep `run_path`, append `- <today> Spec v6.1 changed this case; earlier results superseded.`); run notes = repo notes ∪ vault notes (deduplicated, order kept); `requirement:` is always written as `"[[REQ-xxx]]"` (double quotes — `scripts/nfr-traceability.test.mjs` l.16 only matches that form; 23 vault notes use single quotes today); `.DS_Store` and `traceability-matrix.xlsx` are skipped; `traceability-dashboard.md` = vault text + the repo section from `## INT-81 snapshot` to EOF with the count table regenerated from the merged UAT notes.
**Covers:** NFR-010 (traceability), REQ-005-style "never lose evidence".

  ```js
  test("NFR-010 unchanged case keeps Pass and notes", () => {
  ```

**Files:** `docs/requirements/**` (generated); create `docs/testing/v6.1-rerun.md`.

Copy procedure (the vault is the source; never edit `docs/requirements/` by hand):
1. Gate: `grep -c "draft v6.1" "$VAULT/requirements.md"` → `1` (today the vault says `draft v6`; wait for the spec owner).
2. Clean the source in the vault, not in the repo: remove brain question ids and the `docs/project-context/` path from published notes (`REQ-003` l.13/20 `(Q-031)`, `REQ-008` l.15/23 `See Q-003.`, `REQ-052` l.17/27 `→ Q-034`, `requirements.md` l.3 "the documents in `docs/project-context/`" → "the project source documents (GA, D2.5, D3.1, D5.5 scope, D6.1)"). Check: `grep -rnoP "(?<![A-Z])Q-\d{3}|project-context|brain/" "$VAULT"` → no output.

---

#### Review focus (Workstream 6)

1. **Sync overwrites run evidence.** A naive `cp -r` from the vault resets all 79 UAT notes to `Not Run` and drops `## Run notes` (vault statuses are reset). Covered by `mergeUatNote` cases "unchanged case keeps Pass and notes" and "changed then resets status" (Task 6.6).
2. **Single-quoted `requirement:` breaks traceability silently.** `nfr-traceability.test.mjs` l.16 matches only `"[[…]]"`; 23 vault notes use `'[[…]]'`, so their REQs would be reported as uncovered (or, worse, the test edited to pass). Covered by the "changed then …" assertion `^requirement: "\[\[REQ-001\]\]"$` (Task 6.6).
3. **Brain ids / confidential paths leak through the spec.** `REQ-003`, `REQ-008`, `REQ-052` and `requirements.md` l.3 carry `Q-0xx` or `docs/project-context/`. Covered by `findConfidentialMarkers` (6.6) and the `brain-question-id` rule in `public-hygiene.test.mjs` "HEAD is clean" (6.2).
4. **Docs promise env switches or endpoints that code does not have.** The configuration loop (6.4) and the §3.1 path-vs-contract loop (6.10) fail when a documented env var or path is missing from code/contract.
5. **Hard-coded "62 Pass" survives the re-run.** `release-docs.test.mjs` l.73-74 and `uat-package.test.mjs` l.6-11 pin old numbers; Task 6.14 replaces them with computed counts and a link to `v6.1-rerun.md`.

#### Dependencies

Needs:
- **Spec owner (vault):** spec v6.1 published (`draft v6.1` in `requirements.md`), and the vault cleaned of `Q-0xx` / `docs/project-context/` in published notes (Task 6.7 step 2).
- **EWDS-integration workstream:** final names of the five records (`billingStatement`, `billingInvoice`, `billingPayment`, `billingPayout`, `billingKycStatus`), the command channel and commands (`startPayment`, `requestWithdrawal`, `setInvoiceStatus`, `startKyc`, `sendReminder`, `triggerBillingRun`), their schema files under `docs/architecture/ewds-topics/`, and the adapter environment variable names in `services/*` and charts.
- **Billing-model workstream:** statement/invoice/threshold contracts and events (StatementIssued or equivalent), the removal of `POST /v1/early-settlements` and how `/v1` versioning handles it, new mock scenarios S14+ in `mocks/README.md`, removal of `STRIPE_INVOICE_PROVIDER`, pdf-lib adoption.
- **Payments/KYC workstream:** payout and KYC contracts, events and lifecycle states, PSP-neutral port names, the card + SEPA decision implemented.
- **GDPR/identity workstream:** real IdP/PSP erasure adapters (to move UAT-013 off `run_path: mock`).
- **Release workstream:** the tag to cite (`VERSIONING.md` / `cut-release.mjs`) and the bumped `package.json` version used by `release-docs.test.mjs`.
- **UAT re-run campaign (all workstreams):** results for the 22 in-scope UATs in `docs/testing/v6.1-rerun.md`.

Provides:
- `scripts/sync-spec.mjs` (`syncSpec`, `mergeUatNote`, `findConfidentialMarkers`) and `RUN_FIELDS` incl. `run_path` — every workstream records runs through these fields.
- `scripts/public-hygiene.mjs` CI guard.
- BR-14 / BR-15, F4.5 / F5.5 / F6.5, P6, P-CMD, P-TARIFF ids in the architecture views, for code comments and test names.
- `docs/testing/v6.1-rerun.md` (re-run list) and computed UAT counts for README, release notes and the report.
- D5.5 report v1.0 (INT-90/INT-91) with the DEV-01…DEV-14 deviation table.
